}

=== FILE: frontend/src/lib/useMe.ts ===
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useAuth } from "@clerk/react";

import { apiGet } from "@/lib/api";
import type { Me } from "@/lib/adminTypes";

// The signed-in person's role. UI hint only — the server checks the role on every call.
export function useMe() {
  const { isSignedIn } = useAuth();
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["me"],
    queryFn: () => apiGet<Me>("/me"),
    enabled: !!isSignedIn,
    staleTime: 60_000,
  });
}

=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/AuthPage.tsx ===
import { Navigate, useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { SignIn, SignUp, useAuth } from "@clerk/react";
import { Recycle, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";

// Same-origin redirect target (e.g. /admin) from ?redirect_url=..., else the citizen home.
function safeTarget(raw: string | null): string {
  if (!raw) return "/";
  try {
    const url = new URL(raw, window.location.origin);
    return url.origin === window.location.origin ? url.pathname + url.search : "/";
  } catch {
    return "/";
  }
}

export default function AuthPage({ mode }: { mode: "sign-in" | "sign-up" }) {
  const { isLoaded, isSignedIn } = useAuth();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const target = safeTarget(params.get("redirect_url"));

  if (isLoaded && isSignedIn) return <Navigate to={target} replace />;

  return (
    <div className="grid min-h-svh lg:grid-cols-2">

            Built with Sahyadri Nisarg Mitra in mind
          </p>
        </div>

            <Recycle className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden />
          </span>
          <p className="font-heading text-lg font-bold text-emerald-950">Swachh Chiplun</p>
        </div>
        {mode === "sign-in" ? (
          <SignIn
            routing="path"
            path="/sign-in"
            signUpUrl="/sign-up"
            withSignUp
            forceRedirectUrl={target}
          />
        ) : (
          <SignUp routing="path" path="/sign-up" signInUrl="/sign-in" forceRedirectUrl={target} />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );

=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/Chat.tsx ===
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useMutation, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { MapPin, Send } from "lucide-react";

import { apiGet, apiPost } from "@/lib/api";
import {
  CATEGORY_META,
  asCategory,
  type ChatHistoryMessage,
  type ChatResponse,
  type Classification,
  type Facility,
  type Language,
} from "@/lib/types";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const QUICK_PROMPTS: Record<Language, string[]> = {
  en: [
    "What do I do with a broken tube light?",
    "Is a PET bottle recyclable?",
    "Where can I dispose of old batteries?",
    "How do I start composting at home?",
  ],
  hi: [
    "टूटी ट्यूबलाइट का क्या करें?",
    "क्या PET बोतल रिसाइकल होती है?",
    "पुरानी बैटरियाँ कहाँ दूँ?",
    "घर पर कम्पोस्टिंग कैसे शुरू करें?",
  ],
  mr: [
    "तुटलेल्या ट्यूबलाइटचे काय करावे?",
    "PET बाटली रीसायकल होते का?",
    "जुन्या बॅटरी कुठे द्याव्यात?",
    "घरी कंपोस्टिंग कसे सुरू करावे?",
  ],
};

const PLACEHOLDERS: Record<Language, string> = {
  en: "Ask about segregation, recycling, e-waste laws, composting…",
  hi: "कचरे के बँटवारे, रिसाइक्लिंग, ई-वेस्ट कानून के बारे में पूछें…",
  mr: "कचऱ्याची विभागणी, रीसायक्लिंग, ई-वेस्ट कायदे याविषयी विचारा…",
};

const LANG_TABS: { value: Language; label: string; testid: string }[] = [
  { value: "en", label: "English", testid: "chat-lang-en" },
  { value: "hi", label: "हिंदी", testid: "chat-lang-hi" },
  { value: "mr", label: "मराठी", testid: "chat-lang-mr" },
];

export default function Chat() {
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState<Language>("en");
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<ChatHistoryMessage[]>([
    {
      role: "assistant",
      content:
        "Namaste! I am Nisarg Sahyak, your waste-help assistant for Chiplun. Ask me about segregation rules, recycling methods, e-waste laws, composting or reuse ideas — or tell me what you scanned and I'll help you dispose of it safely.",
    },
  ]);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("");
  const [facilities, setFacilities] = useState<Facility[]>([]);
  const endRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const lastScan = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["scans", "last"],
    queryFn: () => apiGet<Classification[]>("/scans?limit=1"),
    retry: false,
  });

  const chat = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: {
      message: string;
      language: Language;
      history: ChatHistoryMessage[];
      scan_context: { item_name: string; category: string } | undefined;
    }) => apiPost<ChatResponse>("/chat", body),
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      setMessages((m) => [...m, { role: "assistant", content: data.reply }]);
      setFacilities(data.facilities_used ?? []);
    },
    onError: () =>
      setMessages((m) => [
        ...m,
        { role: "assistant", content: "Sorry — the assistant is unreachable right now. Please try again." },
      ]),
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    endRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "nearest" });
  }, [messages, chat.isPending]);

  const send = (text: string) => {
    const trimmed = text.trim();
    if (!trimmed || chat.isPending) return;
    const last = lastScan.data?.[0];
    setMessages((m) => [...m, { role: "user", content: trimmed }]);
    setDraft("");
    chat.mutate({
      message: trimmed,
      language,
      history: messages.slice(-8),
      scan_context: last ? { item_name: last.item_name, category: last.category } : undefined,
    });
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-3">
      <Card className="flex h-[70vh] min-h-96 flex-col border-emerald-950/10 lg:col-span-2">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-emerald-950/10 px-4 py-3">
          <p className="font-heading text-sm font-bold text-emerald-950">Nisarg Sahyak · AI waste assistant</p>
          <div className="flex gap-1" role="tablist" aria-label="Assistant language">
            {LANG_TABS.map((tab) => (
              <button
                key={tab.value}
                type="button"
                role="tab"
                aria-selected={language === tab.value}
                data-selected={language === tab.value}
                onClick={() => setLanguage(tab.value)}
                data-testid={tab.testid}
                className={cn(
                  "rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold transition",
                  language === tab.value
                    ? "bg-emerald-700 text-white"
                    : "bg-[#F4F7F4] text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-100",
                )}
              >
                {tab.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4">
          {messages.map((msg, i) => (
            <div
              key={i}
              data-testid={`chat-bubble-${i}`}
              className={cn(
                "max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-sm leading-relaxed",
                msg.role === "user"
                  ? "ml-auto rounded-br-sm bg-emerald-700 text-white"
                  : "rounded-bl-sm border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white text-emerald-950/90",
              )}
            >
              {msg.content}
            </div>
          ))}
          {chat.isPending && (
            <div className="max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl rounded-bl-sm border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm text-emerald-950/50" data-testid="chat-typing">
              Thinking…
            </div>
          )}
          <div ref={endRef} />
        </div>

        {facilities.length > 0 && (
          <div className="border-t border-emerald-950/10 px-4 py-3" data-testid="chat-facilities">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-800">
              Facilities the assistant found
            </p>
            <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {facilities.map((f) => (
                <Link
                  key={f.id}
                  to="/facilities"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-emerald-900/15 bg-[#F4F7F4] px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-50"
                  data-testid={`chat-facility-${f.id}`}
                >
                  <MapPin className="h-3 w-3" aria-hidden />
                  {f.name}
                  {f.distance_m != null && ` · ${(f.distance_m / 1000).toFixed(1)} km`}
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex gap-2 border-t border-emerald-950/10 p-3">
          <Input
            value={draft}
            onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)}
            onKeyDown={(e) => {
              if (e.key === "Enter" && !e.shiftKey) {
                e.preventDefault();
                send(draft);
              }
            }}
            placeholder={PLACEHOLDERS[language]}
            data-testid="chat-input"
          />
          <Button onClick={() => send(draft)} disabled={chat.isPending || !draft.trim()} data-testid="chat-send">
            <Send className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
            <span className="sr-only">Send</span>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Card>

      <div className="space-y-4">
        <Card size="sm" className="border-emerald-950/10">
          <CardContent className="pt-5">
            <h2 className="font-heading text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-800">
              Try asking
            </h2>
            <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
              {QUICK_PROMPTS[language].map((prompt, i) => (
                <button
                  key={prompt}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => send(prompt)}
                  disabled={chat.isPending}
                  data-testid={`chat-quick-${i}`}
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-emerald-900/10 bg-[#F4F7F4] px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-sm text-emerald-900 transition hover:border-emerald-700/40 hover:bg-emerald-50"
                >
                  {prompt}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        {lastScan.data?.[0] && (
          <Card size="sm" className="border-emerald-950/10">
            <CardContent className="pt-5">
              <h2 className="font-heading text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-800">
                Context from your last scan
              </h2>
              <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <span
                  className={`inline-flex rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${
                    CATEGORY_META[asCategory(lastScan.data[0].category)].pill
                  }`}
                  data-testid="chat-context-pill"
                >
                  {CATEGORY_META[asCategory(lastScan.data[0].category)].label}
                </span>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-emerald-950">{lastScan.data[0].item_name}</p>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-emerald-950/60">
                The assistant already knows what you scanned — ask "where do I take this?" for
                nearby facilities.
              </p>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx ===
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Leaf, ScanLine, Trash2, Trophy } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Cell, Pie, PieChart, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip } from "recharts";
import { format } from "date-fns";

import { apiDelete, apiGet } from "@/lib/api";
import {
  CATEGORY_META,
  CATEGORY_ORDER,
  asCategory,
  type Classification,
  type ScanDeleteResponse,
  type Stats,
} from "@/lib/types";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table";

export default function Dashboard() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const stats = useQuery({ queryKey: ["stats"], queryFn: () => apiGet<Stats>("/stats") });
  const scans = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["scans"],
    queryFn: () => apiGet<Classification[]>("/scans?limit=50"),
  });

  const removeScan = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => apiDelete<ScanDeleteResponse>(`/scans/${id}`),
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["scans"] });
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["stats"] });
      toast.success("Scan deleted");
    },
    onError: () => toast.error("Could not delete the scan"),
  });

  const byCategory = stats.data?.by_category ?? {};
  const pieData = CATEGORY_ORDER.filter((c) => (byCategory[c] ?? 0) > 0).map((c) => ({
    name: CATEGORY_META[c].label,
    value: byCategory[c],
    hex: CATEGORY_META[c].hex,
  }));
  const topCategory =
    pieData.length > 0 ? pieData.reduce((a, b) => (a.value >= b.value ? a : b)).name : "—";

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <Card size="sm" className="border-emerald-950/10" data-testid="dashboard-stat-scans">
          <CardContent className="pt-5">
            <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-700/10 text-emerald-700">
              <ScanLine className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden />
            </span>
            <p className="mt-3 font-heading text-3xl font-bold text-emerald-950">
              {stats.data?.total_scans ?? "—"}
            </p>
            <p className="text-sm text-emerald-950/60">Items scanned</p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card size="sm" className="border-emerald-950/10" data-testid="dashboard-stat-points">
          <CardContent className="pt-5">
            <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-600">
              <Trophy className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden />
            </span>
            <p className="mt-3 font-heading text-3xl font-bold text-emerald-950">
              {stats.data?.eco_points ?? "—"}
            </p>
            <p className="text-sm text-emerald-950/60">Eco points earned</p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card size="sm" className="border-emerald-950/10" data-testid="dashboard-stat-top">
          <CardContent className="pt-5">
            <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-700/10 text-emerald-700">
              <Leaf className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden />
            </span>
            <p className="mt-3 font-heading text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-950" data-testid="dashboard-top-category">
              {topCategory}
            </p>
            <p className="text-sm text-emerald-950/60">Most scanned category</p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-5">
        <Card className="border-emerald-950/10 lg:col-span-2">
          <CardContent className="pt-5">
            <h2 className="font-heading text-base font-bold tracking-tight text-emerald-950">
              Waste composition
            </h2>
            {pieData.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="mt-6 text-center" data-testid="dashboard-empty">
                <p className="text-sm text-emerald-950/60">No scans yet.</p>
                <Button className="mt-3" render={<Link to="/scan" />} data-testid="dashboard-empty-cta">
                  Scan your first item
                </Button>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="mt-2 h-72" data-testid="dashboard-pie-chart">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <PieChart>
                    <Pie data={pieData} dataKey="value" nameKey="name" innerRadius={55} outerRadius={90} paddingAngle={3}>
                      {pieData.map((entry) => (
                        <Cell key={entry.name} fill={entry.hex} stroke="#fff" strokeWidth={2} />
                      ))}
                    </Pie>
                    <Tooltip formatter={(value: number, name: string) => [`${value} scans`, name]} />
                  </PieChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card className="border-emerald-950/10 lg:col-span-3" data-testid="dashboard-history">
          <CardContent className="pt-5">
            <h2 className="font-heading text-base font-bold tracking-tight text-emerald-950">
              Scan history
            </h2>
            {scans.data && scans.data.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="mt-3">
                <Table>
                  <TableHeader>
                    <TableRow>
                      <TableHead>Item</TableHead>
                      <TableHead>Category</TableHead>
                      <TableHead>Confidence</TableHead>
                      <TableHead>Points</TableHead>
                      <TableHead>When</TableHead>
                      <TableHead aria-label="Actions" />
                    </TableRow>
                  </TableHeader>
                  <TableBody>
                    {scans.data.map((scan) => (
                      <TableRow key={scan.id} data-testid={`dashboard-row-${scan.id}`}>
                        <TableCell className="font-medium">{scan.item_name}</TableCell>
                        <TableCell>
                          <span
                            className={`inline-flex rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold ${
                              CATEGORY_META[asCategory(scan.category)].pill
                            }`}
                          >
                            {scan.category_label || CATEGORY_META[asCategory(scan.category)].label}
                          </span>
                        </TableCell>
                        <TableCell>{Math.round(scan.confidence * 100)}%</TableCell>
                        <TableCell>+{scan.eco_points}</TableCell>
                        <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap text-emerald-950/60">
                          {format(new Date(scan.created_at), "dd MMM, HH:mm")}
                        </TableCell>
                        <TableCell>
                          <Button
                            size="icon-sm"
                            variant="ghost"
                            aria-label={`Delete scan ${scan.item_name}`}
                            data-testid={`scan-delete-${scan.id}`}
                            onClick={() => removeScan.mutate(scan.id)}
                          >
                            <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 text-red-600" aria-hidden />
                          </Button>
                        </TableCell>
                      </TableRow>
                    ))}
                  </TableBody>
                </Table>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-emerald-950/60" data-testid="dashboard-history-empty">
                {scans.isLoading ? "Loading history…" : "Scans you make will be listed here."}
              </p>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/Facilities.tsx ===
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Clock, Crosshair, MapPin, Phone, Navigation } from "lucide-react";
import leaflet from "leaflet";
import "leaflet/dist/leaflet.css";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { apiGet } from "@/lib/api";
import { PickupPromoCard } from "@/components/doorstep/DoorstepMap";
import { CATEGORY_META, CATEGORY_ORDER, asCategory, type Category, type Facility } from "@/lib/types";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const CHIPLUN = { lat: 17.5323, lng: 73.5186 };

function escapeHtml(text: string): string {
  return text.replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => `&#${c.charCodeAt(0)};`);
}

export default function Facilities() {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<Category | "all">("all");
  const [coords, setCoords] = useState(CHIPLUN);

  const mapDivRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapRef = useRef<leaflet.Map | null>(null);
  const layerRef = useRef<leaflet.LayerGroup | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mapDivRef.current || mapRef.current) return;
    const map = leaflet.map(mapDivRef.current).setView([CHIPLUN.lat, CHIPLUN.lng], 12);
    leaflet
      .tileLayer("https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png", {
        maxZoom: 19,
        attribution: '&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap</a> contributors',
      })
      .addTo(map);
    layerRef.current = leaflet.layerGroup().addTo(map);
    mapRef.current = map;
    return () => {
      map.remove();
      mapRef.current = null;
      layerRef.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  const query = new URLSearchParams({ lat: String(coords.lat), lng: String(coords.lng), live: "1" });
  if (filter !== "all") query.set("category", filter);

  const facilitiesQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["facilities", filter, coords.lat.toFixed(4), coords.lng.toFixed(4)],
    queryFn: () => apiGet<Facility[]>(`/facilities?${query.toString()}`),
    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,
  });

  const facilities = facilitiesQuery.data ?? [];

  useEffect(() => {
    const layer = layerRef.current;
    if (!layer) return;
    layer.clearLayers();
    for (const f of facilities) {
      leaflet
        .circleMarker([f.lat, f.lng], {
          radius: 9,
          color: "#ffffff",
          weight: 2,
          fillColor: CATEGORY_META[asCategory(f.categories[0] ?? "general")].hex,
          fillOpacity: 0.95,
        })
        .bindPopup(
          `<strong>${escapeHtml(f.name)}</strong><br/>${escapeHtml(f.address)}${
            f.hours ? `<br/><em>${escapeHtml(f.hours)}</em>` : ""
          }${f.phone ? `<br/><a href="tel:${escapeHtml(f.phone)}">${escapeHtml(f.phone)}</a>` : ""}`,
        )
        .addTo(layer);
    }
  }, [facilities]);

  const locate = () => {
    if (!navigator.geolocation) {
      toast.error("Geolocation not supported — showing Chiplun centre");
      return;
    }
    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
      (pos) => {
        setCoords({ lat: pos.coords.latitude, lng: pos.coords.longitude });
        mapRef.current?.setView([pos.coords.latitude, pos.coords.longitude], 13);
        toast.success("Showing facilities near you");
      },
      () => toast.error("Location permission denied — showing Chiplun centre"),
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <PickupPromoCard testid="facilities-pickup-card" />
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2" role="group" aria-label="Filter facilities by waste type">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setFilter("all")}
            data-testid="facilities-filter-all"
            className={cn(
              "rounded-full border px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition",
              filter === "all"
                ? "border-emerald-700 bg-emerald-700 text-white"
                : "border-emerald-900/15 bg-white text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-50",
            )}
          >
            All types
          </button>
          {CATEGORY_ORDER.map((cat) => (
            <button
              key={cat}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setFilter(cat)}
              data-testid={`facilities-filter-${cat}`}
              className={cn(
                "rounded-full border px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition",
                filter === cat
                  ? "border-emerald-700 bg-emerald-700 text-white"
                  : "border-emerald-900/15 bg-white text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-50",
              )}
            >
              <span className={cn("mr-1.5 inline-block h-2 w-2 rounded-full", CATEGORY_META[cat].dot)} aria-hidden />
              {CATEGORY_META[cat].label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={locate} data-testid="facilities-locate">
          <Crosshair className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> Use my location
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-5">
        <Card className="overflow-hidden border-emerald-950/10 lg:col-span-3">
          <div ref={mapDivRef} className="h-[380px] w-full lg:h-[560px]" data-testid="facilities-map" />
        </Card>

        <div className="space-y-3 lg:col-span-2 lg:max-h-[560px] lg:overflow-y-auto lg:pr-1">
          {facilitiesQuery.isLoading && <p className="text-sm text-emerald-950/60">Loading facilities…</p>}
          {facilitiesQuery.isError && (
            <p className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-800">
              Could not load facilities — check that the backend is running.
            </p>
          )}
          {!facilitiesQuery.isLoading && facilities.length === 0 && !facilitiesQuery.isError && (
            <p className="rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-[#F4F7F4] p-4 text-sm text-emerald-950/70">
              No facilities found for this waste type yet — try another category filter.
            </p>
          )}
          {facilities.map((f) => (
            <Card key={f.id} size="sm" className="lift border-emerald-950/10" data-testid={`facility-card-${f.id}`}>
              <CardContent className="space-y-2.5 pt-5">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <h3 className="font-heading text-sm font-bold leading-snug text-emerald-950">{f.name}</h3>
                  <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-emerald-700/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-800" data-testid={`facility-distance-${f.id}`}>
                    {f.distance_m != null ? `${(f.distance_m / 1000).toFixed(1)} km` : "—"}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  {f.categories.map((c) => (
                    <span key={c} className={`inline-flex rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold ${CATEGORY_META[asCategory(c)].pill}`}>
                      {CATEGORY_META[asCategory(c)].label}
                    </span>
                  ))}
                </div>
                <p className="flex items-start gap-1.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-emerald-950/70">
                  <MapPin className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden /> {f.address}
                </p>
                {f.hours && (
                  <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-emerald-950/70">
                    <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden /> {f.hours}
                  </p>
                )}
                {f.phone && (
                  <a href={`tel:${f.phone}`} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-emerald-700 underline-offset-2 hover:underline" data-testid={`facility-phone-${f.id}`}>
                    <Phone className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden /> {f.phone}
                  </a>
                )}
                {f.accepts.length > 0 && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-emerald-950/55">Accepts: {f.accepts.join(", ")}</p>
                )}
                <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
                  <Button
                    size="sm"
                    render={
                      <a
                        href={`https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&destination=${f.lat},${f.lng}`}
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      />
                    }
                    data-testid={`facility-directions-${f.id}`}
                  >
                    <Navigation className="mr-1 h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden /> Directions
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    size="sm"
                    variant="ghost"
                    render={
                      <a
                        href={`https://www.openstreetmap.org/?mlat=${f.lat}&mlon=${f.lng}#map=17/${f.lat}/${f.lng}`}
                        target="_blank"
                        rel="noopener noreferrer"
                      />
                    }
                    data-testid={`facility-osm-${f.id}`}
                  >
                    View on OSM
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/Home.tsx ===

=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/Pickup.tsx ===
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { BellRing, Crosshair, MapPin, Trash2, Truck, X } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { apiDelete, apiGet, apiPost, apiPut, ApiError } from "@/lib/api";
import { CATEGORY_META, CATEGORY_ORDER } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  ALERT_OPTIONS,
  STATUS_META,
  fmtDistance,
  type AlertM,
  type LatLng,
  type OkResponse,
  type PickupAlert,
  type PickupLive,
  type Spot,
  type SpotIn,
} from "@/lib/doorstepTypes";
import DoorstepMap from "@/components/doorstep/DoorstepMap";
import { useConfirm } from "@/components/admin/AdminUi";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const LIVE_KEY = ["pickup-live"];
const STEPS = ["requested", "near", "collected"] as const;
const errText = (e: unknown) => (e instanceof ApiError || e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Something went wrong");

export default function Pickup() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { confirm, dialog } = useConfirm();
  const live = useQuery({
    queryKey: LIVE_KEY,
    queryFn: () => apiGet<PickupLive>("/pickup/live"),
    refetchInterval: 4000,
  });
  const data = live.data;
  // opened from the bottom of Home/Facilities: start at the top so the map is visible
  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }, []);

  // -------- spot form (draft until saved)
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<LatLng | null>(null);
  const [label, setLabel] = useState("My pickup spot");
  const [alertM, setAlertM] = useState<AlertM>(500);
  const [alertsOn, setAlertsOn] = useState(true);
  const [fit, setFit] = useState("");
  const loadedSpot = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!data || loadedSpot.current) return;
    loadedSpot.current = true;
    if (data.spot) {
      setDraft({ lat: data.spot.lat, lng: data.spot.lng });
      setLabel(data.spot.label);
      setAlertM(([300, 500, 1000].includes(data.spot.alert_m) ? data.spot.alert_m : 500) as AlertM);
      setAlertsOn(data.spot.alerts_on);
    }
    setFit("initial");
  }, [data]);

  const saveSpot = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: SpotIn) => apiPut<Spot>("/pickup/spot", body),
    onSuccess: () => {
      toast.success("Pickup spot saved");
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: LIVE_KEY });
    },
    onError: (e) => toast.error(errText(e)),
  });
  const deleteSpot = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => apiDelete<OkResponse>("/pickup/spot"),
    onSuccess: () => {
      setDraft(null);
      toast.success("Pickup spot deleted");
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: LIVE_KEY });
    },
    onError: (e) => toast.error(errText(e)),
  });

  const useMyLocation = () => {
    if (!navigator.geolocation) return toast.error("Location is not available on this device");
    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
      (pos) => {
        setDraft({ lat: pos.coords.latitude, lng: pos.coords.longitude });
        setFit(`me-${Date.now()}`);
        toast.message("Pin placed at your location — drag it to adjust, then Save");
      },
      () => toast.error("Could not get your location. Click the map to place your pin instead."),
      { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 10000 },
    );
  };

  // -------- "I have waste today"
  const [types, setTypes] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [note, setNote] = useState("");
  const request = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => apiPost<Spot>("/pickup/request", { waste_types: types, note }),
    onSuccess: () => {
      toast.success("Pickup requested — we will alert you when the vehicle is near");
      setNote("");
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: LIVE_KEY });
    },
    onError: (e) => toast.error(errText(e)),
  });
  const cancel = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => apiDelete<OkResponse>("/pickup/request"),
    onSuccess: () => {
      toast.success("Pickup request cancelled");
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: LIVE_KEY });
    },
    onError: (e) => toast.error(errText(e)),
  });

  // -------- alerts: banner + browser notification, once per new alert
  const seen = useRef<Set<string> | null>(null);
  const [banner, setBanner] = useState<PickupAlert | null>(null);
  const [perm, setPerm] = useState<NotificationPermission | "unsupported">(
    typeof Notification === "undefined" ? "unsupported" : Notification.permission,
  );
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!data) return;
    if (seen.current === null) {
      seen.current = new Set(data.alerts.map((a) => a.id)); // old alerts never pop up again
      return;
    }
    const fresh = data.alerts.filter((a) => !seen.current!.has(a.id));
    fresh.forEach((a) => seen.current!.add(a.id));
    if (fresh.length) {
      const a = fresh[0];
      setBanner(a);
      if (perm === "granted") {
        try {
          new Notification("Garbage vehicle is near (Demo)", { body: `${a.text_en}\n${a.text_mr}`, tag: a.id });
        } catch {
          /* some mobile browsers only allow notifications from a service worker */
        }
      }
    }
  }, [data, perm]);
  const askPermission = async () => {
    if (typeof Notification === "undefined") return;
    setPerm(await Notification.requestPermission());
  };

  const spot = data?.spot ?? null;
  const req = spot?.request ?? null;
  const status = data?.status ?? "waiting";
  const unsaved =
    !!draft && (!spot || spot.lat !== draft.lat || spot.lng !== draft.lng || spot.label !== label || spot.alert_m !== alertM || spot.alerts_on !== alertsOn);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {dialog}
      {banner && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          data-testid="pickup-alert-banner"
          className="sticky top-20 z-30 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-2xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-4 shadow-lg animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2"
        >
          <BellRing className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-blue-700" aria-hidden />
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <p className="font-semibold text-blue-950" data-testid="pickup-alert-banner-en">{banner.text_en}</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-blue-900" data-testid="pickup-alert-banner-mr">{banner.text_mr}</p>
          </div>
          <button aria-label="Dismiss alert" onClick={() => setBanner(null)} className="rounded-md p-1 text-blue-800 hover:bg-blue-100" data-testid="pickup-alert-banner-dismiss">
            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-emerald-700">Doorstep garbage vehicle</p>
          <h1 className="flex items-center gap-2 font-heading text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-950 md:text-3xl" data-testid="pickup-title">
            Pickup
            <span className="rounded-full bg-amber-100 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-800" data-testid="pickup-demo-badge">Demo</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            The vehicle on this page is simulated by the admin for a demonstration. Save your spot near the green route, tell us you have waste, and you will be alerted when it is close.
          </p>
        </div>
        {perm !== "unsupported" && perm !== "granted" && (
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={askPermission} data-testid="pickup-enable-notifications-button">
            <BellRing className="h-4 w-4" /> Enable browser alerts
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[1.6fr_1fr]">
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <DoorstepMap
            testid="pickup-map"
            route={data?.vehicle?.path ?? data?.route?.points ?? null}
            vehicle={data?.vehicle ?? null}
            pins={draft ? [{ key: "me", ...draft, color: STATUS_META[status].hex, text: "", tooltip: `${label} (drag to move)`, draggable: true, selected: true }] : []}
            onMapClick={(p) => setDraft(p)}
            onPinDrag={(_k, p) => setDraft(p)}
            fitKey={fit}
          />
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground" data-testid="pickup-map-hint">
            Click anywhere on the map to drop your pin, then drag it to the exact spot. Green line = today's demo route{data?.route ? ` (${data.route.name})` : ""}.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* live vehicle */}
          <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
            <CardContent className="flex items-center gap-4 pt-5">
              <span className={cn("flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl", data?.vehicle ? "bg-[#0D1B13] text-emerald-300" : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-400")}>
                <Truck className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden />
              </span>
              <div className="min-w-0" data-testid="pickup-vehicle-status">
                {data?.vehicle ? (
                  spot && data.eta_min !== null && data.distance_m !== null ? (
                    <>
                      <p className="font-heading text-lg font-semibold text-emerald-950" data-testid="pickup-eta">About {data.eta_min} minute{data.eta_min === 1 ? "" : "s"} away</p>
                      <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground" data-testid="pickup-distance">
                        {fmtDistance(data.distance_m)} from your spot · {data.vehicle.state === "paused" ? "paused" : "on the way"}
                      </p>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <p className="text-sm text-emerald-950">The vehicle is out on its route. Save your spot to see the ETA.</p>
                  )
                ) : (
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">The vehicle is not running right now (demo). Keep this page open.</p>
                )}
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {/* spot form */}
          <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
            <CardContent className="space-y-3 pt-5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <h2 className="font-heading text-base font-semibold text-emerald-950">My pickup spot</h2>
                <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={useMyLocation} data-testid="pickup-use-location-button">
                  <Crosshair className="h-4 w-4" /> Use my location
                </Button>
              </div>
              {!draft && (
                <p className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 p-2 text-sm text-emerald-900" data-testid="pickup-no-spot">
                  <MapPin className="h-4 w-4" /> Click the map to place your pin.
                </p>
              )}
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label htmlFor="spot-label">Label (building / landmark)</Label>
                <Input id="spot-label" value={label} maxLength={80} onChange={(e) => setLabel(e.target.value)} data-testid="pickup-label-input" />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label>Alert me when the vehicle is within</Label>
                <div className="flex gap-2" role="radiogroup">
                  {ALERT_OPTIONS.map((o) => (
                    <button
                      key={o.value}
                      role="radio"
                      aria-checked={alertM === o.value}
                      onClick={() => setAlertM(o.value)}
                      data-testid={`pickup-alert-distance-${o.value}`}
                      className={cn(
                        "flex-1 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-[background-color,color,border-color]",
                        alertM === o.value ? "border-emerald-700 bg-emerald-700 text-white" : "border-emerald-950/15 bg-white hover:bg-emerald-50",
                      )}
                    >
                      {o.label}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-lg border border-emerald-950/10 px-3 py-2">
                <span className="text-sm font-medium">Alerts</span>
                <button
                  role="switch"
                  aria-checked={alertsOn}
                  onClick={() => setAlertsOn((v) => !v)}
                  data-testid="pickup-alerts-switch"
                  className={cn("relative h-6 w-11 shrink-0 rounded-full p-0 transition-colors", alertsOn ? "bg-emerald-600" : "bg-slate-300")}
                >
                  <span className={cn("absolute left-0.5 top-0.5 h-5 w-5 rounded-full bg-white shadow transition-transform", alertsOn ? "translate-x-5" : "translate-x-0")} />
                </button>
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <Button
                  className="flex-1"
                  disabled={!draft || saveSpot.isPending}
                  onClick={() => draft && saveSpot.mutate({ label, lat: draft.lat, lng: draft.lng, alert_m: alertM, alerts_on: alertsOn })}
                  data-testid="pickup-save-spot-button"
                >
                  {spot ? (unsaved ? "Save changes" : "Saved") : "Save spot"}
                </Button>
                {spot && (
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    aria-label="Delete pickup spot"
                    onClick={() => confirm("Delete your pickup spot? Any pickup request is removed too.", () => deleteSpot.mutate())}
                    data-testid="pickup-delete-spot-button"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </Button>
                )}
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>

          {/* request */}
          <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
            <CardContent className="space-y-3 pt-5">
              <h2 className="font-heading text-base font-semibold text-emerald-950">I have waste today</h2>
              {req ? (
                <>
                  <ol className="flex items-center gap-1" data-testid="pickup-status-steps">
                    {STEPS.map((s, i) => {
                      const reached = STEPS.indexOf(req.status) >= i;
                      return (
                        <li key={s} className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-1">
                          <span
                            data-testid={`pickup-step-${s}`}
                            data-reached={reached}
                            className={cn(
                              "flex-1 rounded-lg border px-2 py-1.5 text-center text-xs font-semibold transition-colors",
                              reached ? STATUS_META[s].pill : "border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-400",
                            )}
                          >
                            {STATUS_META[s].label}
                          </span>
                        </li>
                      );
                    })}
                  </ol>
                  <p className="text-sm" data-testid="pickup-request-status">
                    Status: <strong>{STATUS_META[req.status].label}</strong> ·{" "}
                    {req.waste_types.map((t) => CATEGORY_META[t as keyof typeof CATEGORY_META]?.label ?? t).join(", ")}
                    {req.note ? ` · “${req.note}”` : ""}
                  </p>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    {req.status === "collected" && (
                      <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => cancel.mutate()} data-testid="pickup-new-request-button">New request</Button>
                    )}
                    {req.status !== "collected" && (
                      <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => cancel.mutate()} disabled={cancel.isPending} data-testid="pickup-cancel-request-button">
                        Cancel request
                      </Button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    {CATEGORY_ORDER.map((c) => {
                      const on = types.includes(c);
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={c}
                          aria-pressed={on}
                          onClick={() => setTypes((t) => (on ? t.filter((x) => x !== c) : [...t, c]))}
                          data-testid={`pickup-waste-type-${c}`}
                          className={cn(
                            "rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold transition-[background-color,border-color,transform] active:scale-95",
                            on ? CATEGORY_META[c].pill : "border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50",
                          )}
                        >
                          {CATEGORY_META[c].label}
                        </button>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                  <Textarea placeholder="Optional note (e.g. 2 bags, near the gate)" value={note} maxLength={200} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} data-testid="pickup-note-input" />
                  <Button
                    className="w-full"
                    disabled={!spot || types.length === 0 || request.isPending}
                    onClick={() => request.mutate()}
                    data-testid="pickup-request-button"
                  >
                    I have waste today
                  </Button>
                  {!spot && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Save your pickup spot first.</p>}
                </>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* alert history */}
      <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
        <CardContent className="pt-5">
          <h2 className="mb-3 font-heading text-base font-semibold text-emerald-950">My recent alerts</h2>
          {data && data.alerts.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground" data-testid="pickup-alerts-empty">No alerts yet.</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="divide-y divide-emerald-950/5" data-testid="pickup-alerts-list">
              {data?.alerts.map((a) => (
                <li key={a.id} className="py-2 text-sm" data-testid="pickup-alert-item">
                  <p className="text-emerald-950">{a.text_en}</p>
                  <p className="text-muted-foreground">{a.text_mr}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {new Date(a.at).toLocaleString()} · {fmtDistance(a.distance_m)}
                  </p>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}


=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/Scan.tsx ===
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Barcode, Camera, CameraOff, CircleAlert, ScanLine, Sparkles, Upload } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { apiPost } from "@/lib/api";
import {
  CATEGORY_META,
  LANGUAGES,
  asCategory,
  type Classification,
  type Language,
} from "@/lib/types";
import { Button, buttonVariants } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

type Detector = { detect: (src: CanvasImageSource) => Promise<{ rawValue: string }[]> };
const getDetector = (): Detector | null => {
  const Ctor = (window as unknown as { BarcodeDetector?: new () => Detector }).BarcodeDetector;
  return Ctor ? new Ctor() : null;
};

const PRESETS = ["Old mobile phone", "PET water bottle", "Broken tube light", "Kitchen vegetable peels", "Cardboard box"];

export default function Scan() {
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState<Language>("en");
  const [hint, setHint] = useState("");
  const [image, setImage] = useState<{ base64: string; mime: string } | null>(null);
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [cameraOn, setCameraOn] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<Classification | null>(null);

  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
  const streamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null);
  const qc = useQueryClient();

  useEffect(() => () => streamRef.current?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop()), []);

  const classify = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: { image_base64: string | null; mime_type: string; hint: string | null; language: Language }) =>
      apiPost<Classification>("/classify", body),
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      setResult(data);
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["scans"] });
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["stats"] });
      if (!data.needs_follow_up) {
        toast.success(`Identified: ${data.item_name} (+${data.eco_points} eco points)`);
      }
    },
    onError: (err) =>
      toast.error(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Classification failed — try again"),
  });

  const runClassify = (opts: { hint?: string; image?: { base64: string; mime: string } | null }) => {
    const hintValue = (opts.hint ?? hint).trim();
    const imageValue = opts.image === undefined ? image : opts.image;
    if (!hintValue && !imageValue) {
      toast.error("Capture a photo or type a hint first");
      return;
    }
    classify.mutate({
      image_base64: imageValue?.base64 ?? null,
      mime_type: imageValue?.mime ?? "image/jpeg",
      hint: hintValue || null,
      language,
    });
  };

  const startCamera = async () => {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        video: { facingMode: { ideal: "environment" } },
        audio: false,
      });
      streamRef.current = stream;
      if (videoRef.current) {
        videoRef.current.srcObject = stream;
        await videoRef.current.play();
      }
      setCameraOn(true);
    } catch {
      toast.error("Camera unavailable — upload a photo instead");
    }
  };

  const stopCamera = () => {
    streamRef.current?.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop());
    streamRef.current = null;
    setCameraOn(false);
  };

  const grabFrame = (source: HTMLVideoElement | HTMLImageElement, w: number, h: number) => {
    const max = 1024;
    const scale = Math.min(1, max / Math.max(w, h));
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    canvas.width = Math.round(w * scale);
    canvas.height = Math.round(h * scale);
    canvas.getContext("2d")?.drawImage(source, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    return canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.85);
  };

  const capture = () => {
    const video = videoRef.current;
    if (!video || !video.videoWidth) return;
    const dataUrl = grabFrame(video, video.videoWidth, video.videoHeight);
    setImage({ base64: dataUrl.split(",")[1] ?? "", mime: "image/jpeg" });
    setPreview(dataUrl);
    setResult(null);
    toast.success("Frame captured — now classify it");
  };

  const onFile = (file: File | undefined) => {
    if (!file) return;
    if (!file.type.startsWith("image/")) {
      toast.error("Please choose a photo (JPG, PNG or WEBP)");
      return;
    }
    if (file.size > 15 * 1024 * 1024) {
      toast.error("That photo is too large (max 15 MB) — choose a smaller one");
      return;
    }
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    const img = new Image();
    img.onload = () => {
      const dataUrl = grabFrame(img, img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight);
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      setImage({ base64: dataUrl.split(",")[1] ?? "", mime: "image/jpeg" });
      setPreview(dataUrl);
      setResult(null);
    };
    img.src = url;
  };

  const scanBarcode = async () => {
    const detector = getDetector();
    const video = videoRef.current;
    if (!detector || !video) return;
    try {
      const codes = await detector.detect(video);
      if (codes.length > 0 && codes[0].rawValue) {
        const value = `Barcode: ${codes[0].rawValue}`;
        setHint(value);
        toast.success("Barcode detected");
      } else {
        toast.error("No barcode found in frame");
      }
    } catch {
      toast.error("Barcode scan failed");
    }
  };

  const answerFollowUp = (option: string) => {
    const combined = [hint.trim(), option].filter(Boolean).join(". ");
    setHint(combined);
    runClassify({ hint: combined });
  };

  const meta = result ? CATEGORY_META[asCategory(result.category)] : null;
  const hasDetector = getDetector() !== null;

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <Card className="overflow-hidden border-emerald-950/10">
          <div className="relative aspect-[4/3] w-full bg-[#0D1B13]">
            <video
              ref={videoRef}
              playsInline
              muted
              className={cn("h-full w-full object-cover", !cameraOn && "hidden")}
              data-testid="scan-camera-video"
            />
            {!cameraOn && preview && (
              <img src={preview} alt="Captured waste item" className="h-full w-full object-cover" data-testid="scan-preview" />
            )}
            {!cameraOn && !preview && (
              <div className="flex h-full w-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 text-emerald-100/70">
                <ScanLine className="h-12 w-12 animate-pulse" aria-hidden />
                <p className="text-sm">Start the camera or upload a photo</p>
              </div>
            )}
            {cameraOn && (
              <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-8 rounded-xl border-2 border-emerald-300/70 animate-pulse" aria-hidden />
            )}
          </div>
          <CardContent className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 pt-4">
            {cameraOn ? (
              <Button variant="outline" onClick={stopCamera} data-testid="scan-camera-stop">
                <CameraOff className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> Stop camera
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <Button onClick={startCamera} data-testid="scan-camera-start">
                <Camera className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> Start camera
              </Button>
            )}
            <Button onClick={capture} disabled={!cameraOn} data-testid="scan-capture">
              <ScanLine className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> Capture
            </Button>
            <label className={buttonVariants({ variant: "outline" })}>
              <Upload className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> Upload
              <input
                type="file"
                accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp"
                className="hidden"
                onChange={(e) => onFile(e.target.files?.[0])}
                data-testid="scan-upload-input"
              />
            </label>
            {hasDetector && cameraOn && (
              <Button variant="ghost" onClick={scanBarcode} data-testid="scan-barcode">
                <Barcode className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> Barcode
              </Button>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle className="flex items-center justify-between font-heading text-base">
              Optional hint &amp; language
              <Select value={language} onValueChange={(v: string) => setLanguage(v as Language)}>
                <SelectTrigger size="sm" className="w-32" data-testid="scan-language">
                  <SelectValue>{(v: string) => LANGUAGES.find((l) => l.value === v)?.label ?? v}</SelectValue>
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  {LANGUAGES.map((l) => (
                    <SelectItem key={l.value} value={l.value} data-testid={`scan-language-${l.value}`}>
                      {l.label}
                    </SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </CardTitle>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
            <Input
              value={hint}
              onChange={(e) => setHint(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g. broken tube light from my kitchen / Barcode: 8901234567890"
              data-testid="scan-hint"
            />
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {PRESETS.map((preset, i) => (
                <button
                  key={preset}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setHint(preset);
                    setImage(null);
                    setPreview(null);
                    runClassify({ hint: preset, image: null });
                  }}
                  data-testid={`scan-preset-${i}`}
                  className="rounded-full border border-emerald-900/15 bg-[#F4F7F4] px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-800 transition hover:border-emerald-700/40 hover:bg-emerald-50"
                >
                  <Sparkles className="mr-1 inline h-3 w-3" aria-hidden />
                  {preset}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <Button
              className="w-full"
              onClick={() => runClassify({})}
              disabled={classify.isPending}
              data-testid="scan-classify"
            >
              <ScanLine className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
              {classify.isPending ? "Identifying…" : "Identify waste item"}
            </Button>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <div>
        {result && meta ? (
          <Card className="border-emerald-950/10" data-testid="scan-result">
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 font-heading">
                <span className={`inline-flex rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${meta.pill}`}>
                  {result.category_label || meta.label}
                </span>
                <span className="text-xl font-bold">{result.item_name}</span>
              </CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-5">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-emerald-950/70">
                  <span>Confidence</span>
                  <span className="font-semibold">{Math.round(result.confidence * 100)}%</span>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-1.5 h-2 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-muted">
                  <div
                    className="h-full rounded-full bg-emerald-600 transition-all"
                    style={{ width: `${Math.round(result.confidence * 100)}%` }}
                    data-testid="scan-confidence-fill"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <span className="rounded-full bg-emerald-700 px-3 py-1 font-semibold text-white" data-testid="scan-eco-points">
                  +{result.eco_points} eco points
                </span>
                {result.subcategory && (
                  <span className="text-emerald-950/60">Type: {result.subcategory}</span>
                )}
              </div>

              {result.hazards.length > 0 && (
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4">
                  <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-red-800">
                    <CircleAlert className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> Handle with care
                  </p>
                  <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1.5">
                    {result.hazards.map((hazard, i) => (
                      <li key={i} className="text-sm text-red-800/90" data-testid={`scan-hazard-${i}`}>
                        • {hazard}
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              )}

              <div>
                <h3 className="font-heading text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-800">
                  How to dispose of it
                </h3>
                <ol className="mt-2 space-y-2">
                  {result.disposal_steps.map((step, i) => (
                    <li key={i} className="flex gap-2.5 text-sm leading-relaxed" data-testid={`scan-step-${i}`}>
                      <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-emerald-700 text-[11px] font-bold text-white">
                        {i + 1}
                      </span>
                      {step}
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ol>
              </div>

              {result.eco_tip && (
                <p className="rounded-xl bg-[#F4F7F4] p-3 text-sm italic text-emerald-950/75" data-testid="scan-eco-tip">
                  Eco tip: {result.eco_tip}
                </p>
              )}

              {result.needs_follow_up && result.follow_up_question && (
                <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4" data-testid="scan-followup">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-800">{result.follow_up_question}</p>
                  <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    {result.follow_up_options.map((option, i) => (
                      <button
                        key={i}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => answerFollowUp(option)}
                        data-testid={`scan-followup-option-${i}`}
                        className="rounded-full border border-amber-300 bg-white px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-amber-800 transition hover:bg-amber-100"
                      >
                        {option}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <Button variant="outline" render={<Link to="/facilities" />} className="w-full" data-testid="scan-result-map-link">
                Find the nearest facility <span aria-hidden>→</span>
              </Button>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <Card className="flex h-full min-h-72 items-center justify-center border-dashed border-emerald-950/15 bg-[#F4F7F4]/60">
            <CardContent className="py-10 text-center">
              <ScanLine className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-emerald-700/50" aria-hidden />
              <p className="mt-4 max-w-xs text-sm leading-relaxed text-emerald-950/60">
                Your result appears here — item name, category, confidence, disposal steps and eco
                points. No photo handy? Tap a sample preset below the hint box to see it work.
              </p>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminAudit.tsx ===
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { format } from "date-fns";

import { apiGet } from "@/lib/api";
import type { AuditEntry } from "@/lib/adminTypes";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "@/components/ui/table";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/admin/AdminUi";

const ACTION_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  create_category: "Added category",
  update_category: "Edited category",
  delete_category: "Deleted category",
  create_facility: "Added facility",
  update_facility: "Edited facility",
  activate_facility: "Activated facility",
  deactivate_facility: "Deactivated facility",
  delete_facility: "Deleted facility",
  delete_record: "Deleted waste record",
  change_role: "Changed user role",
  reply_feedback: "Replied to feedback",
  delete_feedback: "Deleted feedback",
};

export default function AdminAudit() {
  const log = useQuery({ queryKey: ["admin", "audit"], queryFn: () => apiGet<AuditEntry[]>("/admin/audit?limit=200") });

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Audit log" subtitle="Every change made in the admin panel — who did what, and when. It cannot be edited." />
      <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
        <CardContent className="pt-5">
          <Table>
            <TableHeader>
              <TableRow>
                <TableHead>When</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Admin</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Action</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Target</TableHead>
              </TableRow>
            </TableHeader>
            <TableBody>
              {(log.data ?? []).map((a) => (
                <TableRow key={a.id} data-testid={`audit-row-${a.id}`}>
                  <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap text-emerald-950/60">{format(new Date(a.at), "dd MMM yyyy, HH:mm")}</TableCell>
                  <TableCell>{a.admin_email}</TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="font-medium">{ACTION_LABEL[a.action] ?? a.action}</TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="max-w-72 truncate">{a.target}</TableCell>
                </TableRow>
              ))}
            </TableBody>
          </Table>
          {log.data?.length === 0 && (
            <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-emerald-950/60" data-testid="audit-empty">No admin actions yet.</p>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </>
  );
}


=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminDashboard.tsx ===
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Bar, BarChart, CartesianGrid, Cell, Pie, PieChart, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis } from "recharts";
import { formatDistanceToNow } from "date-fns";
import { MessageSquareWarning, ScanLine, ShieldCheck, UserPlus } from "lucide-react";

import { apiGet } from "@/lib/api";
import type { AdminGate, AdminStats } from "@/lib/adminTypes";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { PageHeader, StatCard } from "@/components/admin/AdminUi";
import { useGuidelines } from "@/components/admin/useGuidelines";

const ACTIVITY_ICON = { scan: ScanLine, feedback: MessageSquareWarning, signup: UserPlus } as const;

export default function AdminDashboard() {
  const stats = useQuery({ queryKey: ["admin", "stats"], queryFn: () => apiGet<AdminStats>("/admin/stats") });
  const g = useGuidelines();
  const gate = useQuery({ queryKey: ["admin", "gate"], queryFn: () => apiGet<AdminGate>("/admin/gate") });
  const s = stats.data;

  const pieData = Object.entries(s?.by_category ?? {}).map(([key, value]) => ({
    key,
    name: g.label(key),
    value,
    color: g.color(key),
  }));
  const daily = (s?.daily ?? []).map((d) => ({ ...d, label: d.date.slice(5) }));

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Dashboard" subtitle="Live overview of waste identification across Chiplun." />

      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-emerald-700/20 bg-emerald-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-emerald-900" data-testid="admin-account-note">
        <ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
        <p>
          <span className="font-semibold">Admin account:</span> {gate.data?.admin_email ?? "…"} — the only admin of this app
          (set by ADMIN_EMAIL, verified email required).
        </p>
      </div>

      {stats.isError && (
        <p className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-800">Could not load statistics.</p>
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
        <StatCard testid="admin-stat-users" label="Registered users" value={s?.total_users ?? "—"} hint="Citizens + admins who signed in" />
        <StatCard testid="admin-stat-scans" label="Waste identifications" value={s?.total_scans ?? "—"} hint={`${s?.low_confidence_scans ?? 0} needed a follow-up (low confidence)`} />
        <StatCard testid="admin-stat-feedback" label="Open feedback" value={s?.open_feedback ?? "—"} hint={`${s?.total_feedback ?? 0} received in total`} />
        <StatCard testid="admin-stat-facilities" label="Active facilities" value={s ? `${s.active_facilities}/${s.total_facilities}` : "—"} hint={`${s?.total_eco_points ?? 0} eco points earned by citizens`} />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-5">
        <Card className="border-emerald-950/10 lg:col-span-3">
          <CardContent className="pt-5">
            <h2 className="font-heading text-base font-bold text-emerald-950">Identifications — last 14 days</h2>
            <div className="mt-3 h-64" data-testid="admin-daily-chart">
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <BarChart data={daily}>
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e2e8e4" vertical={false} />
                  <XAxis dataKey="label" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
                  <YAxis allowDecimals={false} tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} width={28} />
                  <Tooltip formatter={(value: number) => [`${value} scans`, "Scans"]} labelFormatter={(label: string) => `Date: ${label}`} />
                  <Bar dataKey="count" fill="#15803d" radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} />
                </BarChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card className="border-emerald-950/10 lg:col-span-2">
          <CardContent className="pt-5">
            <h2 className="font-heading text-base font-bold text-emerald-950">Waste category counts</h2>
            {pieData.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="mt-6 text-sm text-emerald-950/60">No identifications yet.</p>
            ) : (
              <>
                <div className="mt-2 h-48" data-testid="admin-category-chart">
                  <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                    <PieChart>
                      <Pie data={pieData} dataKey="value" nameKey="name" innerRadius={45} outerRadius={78} paddingAngle={3}>
                        {pieData.map((d) => (
                          <Cell key={d.key} fill={d.color} stroke="#fff" strokeWidth={2} />
                        ))}
                      </Pie>
                      <Tooltip formatter={(value: number, name: string) => [`${value} scans`, name]} />
                    </PieChart>
                  </ResponsiveContainer>
                </div>
                <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1.5">
                  {pieData.map((d) => (
                    <li key={d.key} className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm" data-testid={`admin-category-count-${d.key}`}>
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: d.color }} />
                        {d.name}
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-semibold">{d.value}</span>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-5">
        <Card className="border-emerald-950/10 lg:col-span-3">
          <CardContent className="pt-5">
            <h2 className="font-heading text-base font-bold text-emerald-950">Recent activity</h2>
            <ul className="mt-3 divide-y divide-emerald-950/8" data-testid="admin-recent-activity">
              {(s?.recent ?? []).map((a, i) => {
                const Icon = ACTIVITY_ICON[a.kind as keyof typeof ACTIVITY_ICON] ?? ScanLine;
                return (
                  <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-3 py-2.5" data-testid={`admin-activity-${i}`}>
                    <span className="mt-0.5 flex h-7 w-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-emerald-700/10 text-emerald-700">
                      <Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden />
                    </span>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <p className="text-sm text-emerald-950/85">{a.text}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-emerald-950/50">{formatDistanceToNow(new Date(a.at), { addSuffix: true })}</p>
                    </div>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
              {s && s.recent.length === 0 && <li className="py-3 text-sm text-emerald-950/60">No activity yet.</li>}
            </ul>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card className="border-emerald-950/10 lg:col-span-2">
          <CardContent className="pt-5">
            <h2 className="font-heading text-base font-bold text-emerald-950">Most identified items</h2>
            <ol className="mt-3 space-y-2" data-testid="admin-top-items">
              {(s?.top_items ?? []).map((t, i) => (
                <li key={t.item_name} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-sm">
                  <span className="truncate">
                    <span className="mr-2 font-semibold text-emerald-700">{i + 1}.</span>
                    {t.item_name}
                  </span>
                  <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-emerald-700/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800">{t.count}</span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}


=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminDoorstep.tsx ===
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Flag, Gauge, MapPinned, Pause, Play, Plus, Route as RouteIcon, Square, Trash2, Truck, Undo2, Users, Wand2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { apiDelete, apiGet, apiPost, apiPut, ApiError } from "@/lib/api";
import { CATEGORY_META, CATEGORY_ORDER, type Facility } from "@/lib/types";
import {
  ALERT_OPTIONS,
  STATUS_META,
  fmtDistance,
  type AdminDoorstepState,
  type AlertM,
  type DemoUser,
  type DemoUserIn,
  type LatLng,
  type OkResponse,
  type Pin,
  type Route,
  type RouteIn,
} from "@/lib/doorstepTypes";
import DoorstepMap, { type MapPin } from "@/components/doorstep/DoorstepMap";
import { PageHeader, useConfirm } from "@/components/admin/AdminUi";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

type Tab = "route" | "users" | "sim" | "driver";
const TABS: { id: Tab; label: string; icon: typeof RouteIcon }[] = [
  { id: "route", label: "Route", icon: RouteIcon },
  { id: "users", label: "Demo users", icon: Users },
  { id: "sim", label: "Vehicle & live map", icon: Truck },
  { id: "driver", label: "Driver view", icon: Flag },
];
const STATE_KEY = ["doorstep-state"];
const ROUTES_KEY = ["doorstep-routes"];
const DEMO_KEY = ["doorstep-demo-users"];
const errText = (e: unknown) => (e instanceof ApiError || e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Something went wrong");
const typeLabels = (t: string[]) => t.map((x) => CATEGORY_META[x as keyof typeof CATEGORY_META]?.label ?? x).join(", ");
const selectCls = "h-9 w-full rounded-lg border border-input bg-white px-3 text-sm";

export default function AdminDoorstep() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { confirm, dialog } = useConfirm();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("sim");

  const routes = useQuery({ queryKey: ROUTES_KEY, queryFn: () => apiGet<Route[]>("/admin/doorstep/routes") });
  const demoUsers = useQuery({ queryKey: DEMO_KEY, queryFn: () => apiGet<DemoUser[]>("/admin/doorstep/demo-users") });
  const facilities = useQuery({ queryKey: ["admin-facilities-doorstep"], queryFn: () => apiGet<Facility[]>("/admin/facilities") });
  const state = useQuery({ queryKey: STATE_KEY, queryFn: () => apiGet<AdminDoorstepState>("/admin/doorstep/state"), refetchInterval: 3000 });
  const live = state.data;
  const running = !!live?.vehicle;

  const [routeId, setRouteId] = useState<string>("");
  const selectedRoute = routes.data?.find((r) => r.id === (routeId || live?.route_id)) ?? routes.data?.[0] ?? null;
  const facility = facilities.data?.find((f) => f.id === selectedRoute?.facility_id) ?? null;

  const refresh = () => {
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: STATE_KEY });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: DEMO_KEY });
    void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ROUTES_KEY });
  };
  const act = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (fn: () => Promise<unknown>) => fn(),
    onSuccess: refresh,
    onError: (e) => toast.error(errText(e)),
  });
  const run = (fn: () => Promise<unknown>, ok?: string) => act.mutate(fn, { onSuccess: () => ok && toast.success(ok) });

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- route tool
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<LatLng[] | null>(null);
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [rName, setRName] = useState("");
  const [rWindow, setRWindow] = useState("07:00 – 09:00");
  const [rFacility, setRFacility] = useState("");
  const startDraft = (r?: Route) => {
    setEditingId(r?.id ?? null);
    setDraft(r ? [...r.points] : []);
    setRName(r?.name ?? "");
    setRWindow(r?.time_window ?? "07:00 – 09:00");
    setRFacility(r?.facility_id ?? "");
    setTab("route");
  };
  const saveRoute = () => {
    if (!draft || draft.length < 2) return toast.error("Click at least a start and an end point on the map");
    if (!rName.trim()) return toast.error("Give the route a name");
    const body: RouteIn = { name: rName.trim(), points: draft, time_window: rWindow, facility_id: rFacility || null };
    run(async () => {
      const saved = editingId ? await apiPut<Route>(`/admin/doorstep/routes/${editingId}`, body) : await apiPost<Route>("/admin/doorstep/routes", body);
      setRouteId(saved.id);
      setDraft(null);
      setEditingId(null);
    }, "Route saved");
  };

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- demo users
  const [addMode, setAddMode] = useState(false);
