  const [newAlert, setNewAlert] = useState<AlertM>(500);
  const [newTypes, setNewTypes] = useState<string[]>(["organic"]);
  const demoById = useMemo(() => new Map((demoUsers.data ?? []).map((d) => [d.id, d])), [demoUsers.data]);
  const demoBody = (d: DemoUser, p?: LatLng): DemoUserIn => ({
    name: d.name,
    label: d.label,
    lat: p?.lat ?? d.lat,
    lng: p?.lng ?? d.lng,
    alert_m: d.alert_m as AlertM,
    waste_types: d.waste_types,
    has_waste: d.has_waste,
  });

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- simulation
  const [speed, setSpeed] = useState<1 | 5 | 10>(5);
  const [pickStart, setPickStart] = useState(false);
  const [customStart, setCustomStart] = useState<LatLng | null>(null);
  const [manual, setManual] = useState(false);
  const [selectedPin, setSelectedPin] = useState<string>("");
  const pins = live?.pins ?? [];
  const selPin = pins.find((p) => p.key === selectedPin) ?? null;

  const onMapClick = (p: LatLng) => {
    if (tab === "route" && draft) setDraft([...draft, p]);
    else if (tab === "users" && addMode)
      run(() => apiPost<DemoUser>("/admin/doorstep/demo-users", { name: "", label: "", lat: p.lat, lng: p.lng, alert_m: newAlert, waste_types: newTypes, has_waste: true }), "Demo user added");
    else if (tab === "sim" && pickStart) {
      setCustomStart(p);
      setPickStart(false);
      toast.message("Start point chosen — press Start");
    }
  };
  const onPinDrag = (key: string, p: LatLng) => {
    const d = demoById.get(key.replace(/^demo_/, ""));
    if (!key.startsWith("demo_") || !d) return;
    run(() => apiPut<DemoUser>(`/admin/doorstep/demo-users/${d.id}`, demoBody(d, p)), `${d.name} moved`);
  };

  const mapPins: MapPin[] = pins.map((p) => ({
    key: p.key,
    lat: p.lat,
    lng: p.lng,
    color: STATUS_META[p.status].hex,
    text: p.kind === "real" ? "R" : p.name.replace(/\D/g, ""),
    square: p.kind === "real",
    draggable: p.kind === "demo" && (tab === "users" || tab === "sim") && !manual,
    selected: p.key === selectedPin,
    tooltip: `${p.kind === "real" ? "Real user · " : ""}${p.name} — ${p.label} · ${STATUS_META[p.status].label} · alert ${fmtDistance(p.alert_m)}${
      p.eta_min ? ` · about ${p.eta_min} min` : ""
    }`,
  }));
  const counts = pins.reduce<Record<string, number>>((acc, p) => ({ ...acc, [p.status]: (acc[p.status] ?? 0) + 1 }), {});
  const routeLine = live?.vehicle?.path ?? selectedRoute?.points ?? null;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {dialog}
      <PageHeader
        title="Doorstep Demo"
        subtitle="DEMO ONLY — simulated garbage vehicle. Demo users and demo trips never change the real dashboard numbers."
        action={
          <span className="rounded-full bg-amber-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-800" data-testid="doorstep-demo-badge">
            Demo
          </span>
        }
      />

      <div className="flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 bg-white p-1" role="tablist">
        {TABS.map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            role="tab"
            aria-selected={tab === t.id}
            onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
            data-testid={`doorstep-tab-${t.id}`}
            className={cn(
              "flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors",
              tab === t.id ? "bg-[#0D1B13] text-white" : "text-emerald-950 hover:bg-emerald-50",
            )}
          >
            <t.icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[1.5fr_1fr]">
        {/* ------------------------------------------------ map (shared by all tabs) */}
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <DoorstepMap
            testid="doorstep-admin-map"
            route={tab === "route" && draft ? null : routeLine}
            draft={tab === "route" ? draft : null}
            vehicle={live?.vehicle ?? null}
            vehicleDraggable={manual && running}
            pins={mapPins}
            facility={facility ? { lat: facility.lat, lng: facility.lng, name: facility.name } : null}
            marker={customStart}
            onMapClick={onMapClick}
            onPinDrag={onPinDrag}
            onPinClick={(k) => setSelectedPin(k)}
            onVehicleDrag={(p) => run(() => apiPut<OkResponse>("/admin/doorstep/trip/position", p))}
            fitKey={`${selectedRoute?.id ?? ""}-${pins.length > 0}`}
            className="relative z-0 h-[380px] w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-emerald-950/10 md:h-[520px]"
          />
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground" data-testid="doorstep-map-legend">
            {(Object.keys(STATUS_META) as (keyof typeof STATUS_META)[]).map((s) => (
              <span key={s} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full" style={{ background: STATUS_META[s].hex }} /> {STATUS_META[s].label} ({counts[s] ?? 0})
              </span>
            ))}
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-[3px] border-2 border-violet-900 bg-slate-400" /> Real user (square, “R”)
            </span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-3 w-3 rounded-full bg-teal-700" /> Drop facility
            </span>
          </div>
          {tab === "route" && draft && (
            <p className="rounded-lg bg-violet-50 p-2 text-sm text-violet-900" data-testid="doorstep-route-hint">
              Click the map: first the <b>start</b>, then each <b>stop in order</b>, then the <b>end</b>. Points so far: {draft.length}
            </p>
          )}
          {tab === "users" && addMode && <p className="rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 p-2 text-sm text-emerald-900">Click the map to add a demo user there.</p>}
          {tab === "sim" && pickStart && <p className="rounded-lg bg-yellow-50 p-2 text-sm text-yellow-900">Click the map to choose the vehicle's start point.</p>}
        </div>

        {/* ------------------------------------------------ side panel */}
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {tab === "route" && (
            <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
              <CardContent className="space-y-3 pt-5">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <Button size="sm" onClick={() => startDraft()} data-testid="doorstep-new-route-button">
                    <Plus className="h-4 w-4" /> New route
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => run(async () => setRouteId((await apiPost<Route>("/admin/doorstep/routes/sample")).id), "Sample route loaded")} data-testid="doorstep-load-sample-route-button">
                    <Wand2 className="h-4 w-4" /> Load sample route
                  </Button>
                </div>
                {draft && (
                  <div className="space-y-3 rounded-xl border border-violet-200 bg-violet-50/40 p-3" data-testid="doorstep-route-form">
                    <div className="space-y-1.5">
                      <Label htmlFor="r-name">Route name</Label>
                      <Input id="r-name" value={rName} onChange={(e) => setRName(e.target.value)} data-testid="doorstep-route-name-input" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-1.5">
                      <Label htmlFor="r-window">Time window</Label>
                      <Input id="r-window" value={rWindow} onChange={(e) => setRWindow(e.target.value)} data-testid="doorstep-route-window-input" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-1.5">
                      <Label htmlFor="r-fac">Drop facility</Label>
                      <select id="r-fac" className={selectCls} value={rFacility} onChange={(e) => setRFacility(e.target.value)} data-testid="doorstep-route-facility-select">
                        <option value="">— none —</option>
                        {facilities.data?.map((f) => (
                          <option key={f.id} value={f.id}>{f.name}</option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      <Button size="sm" onClick={saveRoute} data-testid="doorstep-route-save-button">Save route</Button>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="outline" disabled={!draft.length} onClick={() => setDraft(draft.slice(0, -1))} data-testid="doorstep-route-undo-button">
                        <Undo2 className="h-4 w-4" /> Undo point
                      </Button>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => { setDraft(null); setEditingId(null); }} data-testid="doorstep-route-cancel-button">Cancel</Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
                <ul className="divide-y divide-emerald-950/5" data-testid="doorstep-routes-list">
                  {routes.data?.length === 0 && <li className="py-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">No routes yet — load the sample route.</li>}
                  {routes.data?.map((r) => (
                    <li key={r.id} className={cn("flex items-start gap-2 py-2", selectedRoute?.id === r.id && "bg-emerald-50/60")} data-testid="doorstep-route-item">
                      <button className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-left" onClick={() => setRouteId(r.id)} data-testid="doorstep-route-select-button">
                        <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-950">
                          {r.name}
                          {r.is_demo && <span className="rounded bg-amber-100 px-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-amber-800">Demo</span>}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          {r.points.length - 2} stops · {r.time_window || "no time window"} · drop: {r.facility_name || "none"}
                        </p>
                      </button>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => startDraft(r)} data-testid="doorstep-route-edit-button">Edit</Button>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" aria-label="Delete route" onClick={() => confirm(`Delete route “${r.name}”?`, () => run(() => apiDelete<OkResponse>(`/admin/doorstep/routes/${r.id}`), "Route deleted"))} data-testid="doorstep-route-delete-button">
                        <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                      </Button>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}

          {tab === "users" && (
            <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
              <CardContent className="space-y-3 pt-5">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <Button
                    size="sm"
                    disabled={!selectedRoute}
                    onClick={() => selectedRoute && run(() => apiPost<DemoUser[]>("/admin/doorstep/demo-users/generate", { route_id: selectedRoute.id, count: 12 }), "12 demo users generated along the route")}
                    data-testid="doorstep-generate-demo-users-button"
                  >
                    <Wand2 className="h-4 w-4" /> Generate demo users along the route
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="sm" variant={addMode ? "default" : "outline"} onClick={() => setAddMode((v) => !v)} data-testid="doorstep-add-demo-user-toggle">
                    <MapPinned className="h-4 w-4" /> {addMode ? "Adding: click map" : "Add demo user"}
                  </Button>
                </div>
                {addMode && (
                  <div className="space-y-2 rounded-xl border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/40 p-3">
                    <select className={selectCls} value={newAlert} onChange={(e) => setNewAlert(Number(e.target.value) as AlertM)} data-testid="doorstep-new-demo-alert-select">
                      {ALERT_OPTIONS.map((o) => <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>Alert at {o.label}</option>)}
                    </select>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                      {CATEGORY_ORDER.map((c) => (
                        <button
                          key={c}
                          onClick={() => setNewTypes((t) => (t.includes(c) ? t.filter((x) => x !== c) : [...t, c]))}
                          className={cn("rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold", newTypes.includes(c) ? CATEGORY_META[c].pill : "border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-500")}
                          data-testid={`doorstep-new-demo-type-${c}`}
                        >
                          {CATEGORY_META[c].label}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Drag any numbered demo pin on the map to move it. Demo users cannot log in and have no email or password.</p>
                <ul className="max-h-[420px] divide-y divide-emerald-950/5 overflow-y-auto" data-testid="doorstep-demo-users-list">
                  {demoUsers.data?.length === 0 && <li className="py-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">No demo users yet.</li>}
                  {demoUsers.data?.map((d) => {
                    const st = pins.find((p) => p.key === `demo_${d.id}`)?.status ?? (d.has_waste ? "requested" : "waiting");
                    return (
                      <li key={d.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 py-2" data-testid="doorstep-demo-user-item">
                        <button className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-left" onClick={() => setSelectedPin(`demo_${d.id}`)}>
                          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-950">{d.name} <span className="font-normal text-muted-foreground">· {d.label}</span></p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                            alert {fmtDistance(d.alert_m)} · {d.has_waste ? typeLabels(d.waste_types) : "no waste today"}
                          </p>
                        </button>
                        <span className={cn("rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold", STATUS_META[st].pill)}>{STATUS_META[st].label}</span>
                        <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Delete ${d.name}`} onClick={() => confirm(`Delete ${d.name}?`, () => run(() => apiDelete<OkResponse>(`/admin/doorstep/demo-users/${d.id}`), "Demo user deleted"))} data-testid="doorstep-demo-user-delete-button">
                          <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </Button>
                      </li>
                    );
                  })}
                </ul>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}

          {tab === "sim" && (
            <>
              <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
                <CardContent className="space-y-3 pt-5">
                  <div className="space-y-1.5">
                    <Label htmlFor="sim-route">Route</Label>
                    <select id="sim-route" className={selectCls} value={selectedRoute?.id ?? ""} onChange={(e) => setRouteId(e.target.value)} disabled={running} data-testid="doorstep-sim-route-select">
                      {routes.data?.length === 0 && <option value="">No routes — load the sample route first</option>}
                      {routes.data?.map((r) => <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.name}</option>)}
                    </select>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <span className="font-medium">Start:</span>
                    <Button size="sm" variant={!customStart ? "default" : "outline"} disabled={running} onClick={() => setCustomStart(null)} data-testid="doorstep-start-route-start-button">Route start</Button>
                    <Button size="sm" variant={pickStart || customStart ? "default" : "outline"} disabled={running} onClick={() => setPickStart(true)} data-testid="doorstep-start-pick-map-button">
                      Click on map
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <Gauge className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-800" aria-hidden />
                    <span className="font-medium">Speed:</span>
                    {([1, 5, 10] as const).map((s) => (
                      <button
                        key={s}
                        onClick={() => {
                          setSpeed(s);
                          if (running) run(() => apiPut<OkResponse>("/admin/doorstep/trip/speed", { speed: s }));
                        }}
                        data-testid={`doorstep-speed-${s}x`}
                        className={cn("rounded-lg border px-3 py-1 font-semibold", (live?.vehicle?.speed ?? speed) === s ? "border-emerald-700 bg-emerald-700 text-white" : "border-emerald-950/15 bg-white")}
                      >
                        {s}x
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
                    <Button
                      disabled={!selectedRoute || running}
                      onClick={() => selectedRoute && run(() => apiPost<OkResponse>("/admin/doorstep/trip/start", { route_id: selectedRoute.id, speed, start: customStart }), "Demo trip started")}
                      data-testid="doorstep-start-button"
                    >
                      <Play className="h-4 w-4" /> Start
                    </Button>
                    <Button variant="outline" disabled={!running || live?.vehicle?.state !== "running" || live?.vehicle?.mode === "manual"} onClick={() => run(() => apiPost<OkResponse>("/admin/doorstep/trip/pause"))} data-testid="doorstep-pause-button">
                      <Pause className="h-4 w-4" /> Pause
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      variant="outline"
                      disabled={!running || (live?.vehicle?.state === "running" && live?.vehicle?.mode === "auto")}
                      onClick={() => {
                        setManual(false);
                        run(() => apiPost<OkResponse>("/admin/doorstep/trip/resume"));
                      }}
                      data-testid="doorstep-resume-button"
                    >
                      <Play className="h-4 w-4" /> Resume
                    </Button>
                    <Button variant="destructive" disabled={!running} onClick={() => { setManual(false); run(() => apiPost<OkResponse>("/admin/doorstep/trip/stop"), "Trip stopped"); }} data-testid="doorstep-stop-button">
                      <Square className="h-4 w-4" /> Stop
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="space-y-1.5 border-t border-emerald-950/10 pt-3">
                    <Label htmlFor="sel-user">Selected user (or click a pin)</Label>
                    <select id="sel-user" className={selectCls} value={selectedPin} onChange={(e) => setSelectedPin(e.target.value)} data-testid="doorstep-selected-user-select">
                      <option value="">— choose a user —</option>
                      {pins.map((p) => (
                        <option key={p.key} value={p.key}>{p.kind === "real" ? `Real user · ${p.name}` : p.name} — {STATUS_META[p.status].label}</option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                    <Button variant="outline" className="w-full" disabled={!running || !selPin} onClick={() => selPin && run(() => apiPost<OkResponse>("/admin/doorstep/trip/place-near", { pin_key: selPin.key }), `Vehicle placed about 400 m from ${selPin.name}`)} data-testid="doorstep-place-near-button">
                      <MapPinned className="h-4 w-4" /> Place vehicle near selected user
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <input type="checkbox" className="h-4 w-4 accent-emerald-700" checked={manual}
                      disabled={!running}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const on = e.target.checked;
                        setManual(on);
                        const v = live?.vehicle;
                        // switch the server to manual right away: the vehicle stops where it is
                        if (on && v) run(() => apiPut<OkResponse>("/admin/doorstep/trip/position", { lat: v.lat, lng: v.lng }));
                        if (!on) run(() => apiPost<OkResponse>("/admin/doorstep/trip/resume"));
                      }} data-testid="doorstep-manual-mode-checkbox" />
                    Manual mode — drag the vehicle marker yourself (press Resume to go back to auto)
                  </label>
                  {running && (
                    <div data-testid="doorstep-progress">
                      <div className="mb-1 flex justify-between text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        <span>{live?.vehicle?.state === "paused" ? "Paused" : live?.vehicle?.mode === "manual" ? "Manual mode" : `Driving at ${live?.vehicle?.speed}x`}</span>
                        <span>{live?.progress_pct}% of route</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-emerald-100">
                        <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-emerald-600 transition-[width] duration-700" style={{ width: `${live?.progress_pct ?? 0}%` }} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
              {live?.summary && !running && <SummaryCard s={live.summary} />}
              <FeedCard feed={live?.feed ?? []} />
            </>
          )}

          {tab === "driver" && (
            <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
              <CardContent className="space-y-3 pt-5">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <h2 className="font-heading text-base font-semibold text-emerald-950">Driver view (simulated, read-only)</h2>
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{running ? `${live?.progress_pct}% done` : "No trip running"}</span>
                </div>
                {live?.summary && !running && <SummaryCard s={live.summary} />}
                <ol className="space-y-2" data-testid="doorstep-driver-stops">
                  {running && live?.next_stops.length === 0 && <li className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">No more pickups ahead. Heading to the drop facility.</li>}
                  {live?.next_stops.map((p: Pin, i) => (
                    <li key={p.key} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-emerald-950/10 p-3" data-testid="doorstep-driver-stop">
                      <span className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#0D1B13] text-sm font-bold text-white">{i + 1}</span>
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-950">{p.label || "No label"}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                          {p.kind === "real" ? "Real user · " : ""}{p.name} · {typeLabels(p.waste_types) || "waste"}
                          {p.distance_m !== null ? ` · ${fmtDistance(p.distance_m)}, about ${p.eta_min} min` : ""}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <Button size="sm" onClick={() => run(() => apiPost<OkResponse>("/admin/doorstep/trip/collect", { pin_key: p.key }), `${p.name} collected`)} data-testid="doorstep-driver-collected-button">
                        Collected
                      </Button>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ol>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">The simulation presses “Collected” automatically when the vehicle reaches a stop.</p>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({ s }: { s: NonNullable<AdminDoorstepState["summary"]> }) {
  return (
    <Card className="border-teal-200 bg-teal-50/60" data-testid="doorstep-trip-summary">
      <CardContent className="space-y-1 pt-5">
        <p className="font-heading text-base font-semibold text-teal-950" data-testid="doorstep-dropped-at">Dropped at facility {s.facility_name}</p>
        <p className="text-sm text-teal-900">
          {s.distance_km} km · {s.minutes} min · {s.stops} stops · {s.collected} pickups collected · {s.alerts} alerts sent
        </p>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

function FeedCard({ feed }: { feed: { text: string; at: string }[] }) {
  return (
    <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
      <CardContent className="pt-5">
        <h2 className="mb-2 font-heading text-base font-semibold text-emerald-950">Live alert feed</h2>
        {feed.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Start a trip to see alerts here.</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="max-h-72 space-y-1.5 overflow-y-auto" data-testid="doorstep-alert-feed">
            {feed.map((f, i) => (
              <li key={`${f.at}-${i}`} className="text-sm" data-testid="doorstep-feed-item">
                <span className="mr-2 text-xs tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">{new Date(f.at).toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
                {f.text}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}


=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminFacilities.tsx ===
import { useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Pencil, Plus, Search, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { apiDelete, apiGet, apiPatch, apiPost, apiPut } from "@/lib/api";
import { errorMessage, type AdminFacility, type DeleteResult, type FacilityIn } from "@/lib/adminTypes";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "@/components/ui/table";
import MapPicker from "@/components/admin/MapPicker";
import { CategoryPill, PageHeader, commas, useConfirm } from "@/components/admin/AdminUi";
import { useGuidelines } from "@/components/admin/useGuidelines";

const TYPES = ["E-waste centre", "Recycling unit", "Scrap dealer", "Municipal collection point", "Composting unit", "Hazardous / medical drop-off", "Other"];

interface FormState {
  type: string;
  name: string;
  categories: string[];
  lat: string;
  lng: string;
  address: string;
  phone: string;
  hours: string;
  accepts: string;
  active: boolean;
}

const EMPTY: FormState = {
  type: "",
  name: "",
  categories: [],
  lat: "17.5323",
  lng: "73.5186",
  address: "",
  phone: "",
  hours: "",
  accepts: "",
  active: true,
};

const STATUS = [
  { value: "all", label: "All statuses" },
  { value: "active", label: "Active only" },
  { value: "inactive", label: "Inactive only" },
];

export default function AdminFacilities() {
  const g = useGuidelines();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { confirm, dialog } = useConfirm();
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("");
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("all");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("all");
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<AdminFacility | null>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormState>(EMPTY);
  const set = <K extends keyof FormState>(k: K, v: FormState[K]) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: v }));

  const params = new URLSearchParams({ q });
  if (category !== "all") params.set("category", category);
  if (status !== "all") params.set("status", status);

  const list = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "facilities", params.toString()],
    queryFn: () => apiGet<AdminFacility[]>(`/admin/facilities?${params.toString()}`),
  });

  const refresh = () => {
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["facilities"] }); // citizen map cache
  };

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: FacilityIn) =>
      editing ? apiPut<AdminFacility>(`/admin/facilities/${editing.id}`, body) : apiPost<AdminFacility>("/admin/facilities", body),
    onSuccess: () => {
      refresh();
      setOpen(false);
      toast.success(editing ? "Facility updated" : "Facility added");
    },
    onError: (e) => toast.error(errorMessage(e, "Could not save — check the fields")),
  });

  const toggle = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (f: AdminFacility) => apiPatch<AdminFacility>(`/admin/facilities/${f.id}/active`, { active: !f.active }),
    onSuccess: (f) => {
      refresh();
      toast.success(f.active ? `${f.name} is now visible to citizens` : `${f.name} is hidden from citizens`);
    },
    onError: () => toast.error("Could not change status"),
  });

  const remove = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => apiDelete<DeleteResult>(`/admin/facilities/${id}`),
    onSuccess: () => {
      refresh();
      toast.success("Facility deleted");
    },
    onError: () => toast.error("Could not delete"),
  });

  const openNew = () => {
    setEditing(null);
    setForm(EMPTY);
    setOpen(true);
  };
  const openEdit = (f: AdminFacility) => {
    setEditing(f);
    setForm({
      type: f.type ?? "",
      name: f.name,
      categories: f.categories,
      lat: String(f.lat),
      lng: String(f.lng),
      address: f.address,
      phone: f.phone ?? "",
      hours: f.hours ?? "",
      accepts: f.accepts.join(", "),
      active: f.active,
    });
    setOpen(true);
  };

  const toggleCat = (key: string, on: boolean) =>
    set("categories", on ? [...form.categories, key] : form.categories.filter((c) => c !== key));

  const latNum = Number(form.lat);
  const lngNum = Number(form.lng);
  const valid = form.name.trim().length >= 2 && form.categories.length > 0 && Number.isFinite(latNum) && Number.isFinite(lngNum);

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Facilities & collection points"
        subtitle="Recycling centres, e-waste hubs, scrap dealers and drop-off points citizens see on the map."
        action={
          <Button onClick={openNew} data-testid="facilities-add-button">
            <Plus className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> Add facility
          </Button>
        }
      />

      <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
        <CardContent className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 pt-5">
          <form className="flex min-w-60 flex-1 gap-2" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setQ(draft.trim()); }}>
            <Input value={draft} onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search name or address…" data-testid="admin-facilities-search-input" />
            <Button type="submit" data-testid="admin-facilities-search-button">
              <Search className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
              <span className="sr-only">Search</span>
            </Button>
          </form>
          <Select value={category} onValueChange={(v: string) => setCategory(v)}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-44" data-testid="admin-facilities-category-filter">
              <SelectValue>{(v: string) => (v === "all" ? "All categories" : g.label(v))}</SelectValue>
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              <SelectItem value="all">All categories</SelectItem>
              {(g.data ?? []).map((c) => (
                <SelectItem key={c.key} value={c.key}>{c.name}</SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
          <Select value={status} onValueChange={(v: string) => setStatus(v)}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-40" data-testid="admin-facilities-status-filter">
              <SelectValue>{(v: string) => STATUS.find((s) => s.value === v)?.label ?? v}</SelectValue>
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              {STATUS.map((s) => (
                <SelectItem key={s.value} value={s.value} data-testid={`admin-facilities-status-${s.value}`}>{s.label}</SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
        <CardContent className="pt-5">
          <Table>
            <TableHeader>
              <TableRow>
                <TableHead>Name</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Accepts</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Contact</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Status</TableHead>
                <TableHead aria-label="Actions" />
              </TableRow>
            </TableHeader>
            <TableBody>
              {(list.data ?? []).map((f) => (
                <TableRow key={f.id} className={f.active ? "" : "opacity-60"} data-testid={`admin-facility-row-${f.id}`}>
                  <TableCell>
                    <p className="font-medium">{f.name}</p>
                    {f.type && <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-emerald-700">{f.type}</p>}
                    <p className="max-w-64 truncate text-xs text-emerald-950/55">{f.address}</p>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    <div className="flex max-w-56 flex-wrap gap-1">
                      {f.categories.map((c) => <CategoryPill key={c} label={g.label(c)} color={g.color(c)} />)}
                    </div>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-xs text-emerald-950/70">
                    <p>{f.phone ?? "—"}</p>
                    <p className="max-w-44 truncate">{f.hours ?? ""}</p>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggle.mutate(f)}
                      disabled={toggle.isPending}
                      data-testid={`admin-facility-toggle-${f.id}`}
                      className={`rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
                        f.active ? "border-green-200 bg-green-100 text-green-800 hover:bg-green-200" : "border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-200"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {f.active ? "Active" : "Inactive"}
                    </button>
                    <Badge variant="outline" className="ml-1.5 text-[10px]">{f.source}</Badge>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap text-right">
                    <Button size="icon-sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Edit ${f.name}`} onClick={() => openEdit(f)} data-testid={`admin-facility-edit-${f.id}`}>
                      <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      size="icon-sm"
                      variant="ghost"
                      aria-label={`Delete ${f.name}`}
                      onClick={() => confirm(`Delete "${f.name}" permanently? Tip: deactivate instead to hide it temporarily.`, () => remove.mutate(f.id))}
                      data-testid={`admin-facility-delete-${f.id}`}
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 text-red-600" aria-hidden />
                    </Button>
                  </TableCell>
                </TableRow>
              ))}
            </TableBody>
          </Table>
          {list.data && list.data.length === 0 && (
            <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-emerald-950/60" data-testid="admin-facilities-empty">No facilities match these filters.</p>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-xl" data-testid="facility-form-dialog">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="font-heading">{editing ? `Edit ${editing.name}` : "Add a facility in Chiplun"}</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
              <Label htmlFor="fac-name">Name</Label>
              <Input id="fac-name" value={form.name} onChange={(e) => set("name", e.target.value)} placeholder="Pethmap Ward Dry Waste Centre" data-testid="facility-name-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
              <Label>Type of place</Label>
              <Select value={form.type || "none"} onValueChange={(v: string) => set("type", v === "none" ? "" : v)}>
                <SelectTrigger className="w-full" data-testid="facility-type-select">
                  <SelectValue>{(v: string) => (v === "none" ? "Choose a type" : v)}</SelectValue>
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="none">Choose a type</SelectItem>
                  {TYPES.map((t) => <SelectItem key={t} value={t}>{t}</SelectItem>)}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
              <Label>Waste categories accepted</Label>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                {(g.data ?? []).map((c) => (
                  <label key={c.key} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm">
                    <Checkbox
                      checked={form.categories.includes(c.key)}
                      onCheckedChange={(on: boolean) => toggleCat(c.key, on)}
                      data-testid={`facility-category-${c.key}`}
                    />
                    {c.name}
                  </label>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="fac-lat">Latitude</Label>
              <Input id="fac-lat" inputMode="decimal" value={form.lat} onChange={(e) => set("lat", e.target.value)} data-testid="facility-lat-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="fac-lng">Longitude</Label>
              <Input id="fac-lng" inputMode="decimal" value={form.lng} onChange={(e) => set("lng", e.target.value)} data-testid="facility-lng-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
              <p className="text-xs text-emerald-950/55">Click on the map to set the exact location (or type the numbers above).</p>
              {open && <MapPicker lat={latNum} lng={lngNum} onPick={(la, ln) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, lat: String(la), lng: String(ln) }))} />}
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
              <Label htmlFor="fac-address">Address</Label>
              <Input id="fac-address" value={form.address} onChange={(e) => set("address", e.target.value)} data-testid="facility-address-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="fac-phone">Phone</Label>
              <Input id="fac-phone" value={form.phone} onChange={(e) => set("phone", e.target.value)} data-testid="facility-phone-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="fac-hours">Opening hours</Label>
              <Input id="fac-hours" value={form.hours} onChange={(e) => set("hours", e.target.value)} placeholder="9 AM - 6 PM (Mon-Sat)" data-testid="facility-hours-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
              <Label htmlFor="fac-accepts">Items accepted (comma separated)</Label>
              <Input id="fac-accepts" value={form.accepts} onChange={(e) => set("accepts", e.target.value)} data-testid="facility-accepts-input" />
            </div>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm sm:col-span-2">
              <Checkbox checked={form.active} onCheckedChange={(c: boolean) => set("active", c)} data-testid="facility-active-checkbox" />
              Active — visible on the citizen map and to the AI assistant
            </label>
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setOpen(false)} data-testid="facility-cancel-button">Cancel</Button>
            <Button
              disabled={!valid || save.isPending}
              onClick={() =>
                save.mutate({
                  name: form.name.trim(),
                  categories: form.categories,
                  lat: latNum,
                  lng: lngNum,
                  address: form.address.trim(),
                  phone: form.phone.trim() || null,
                  hours: form.hours.trim() || null,
                  accepts: commas(form.accepts),
                  active: form.active,
                  type: form.type,
                })
              }
              data-testid="facility-save-button"
            >
              {save.isPending ? "Saving…" : "Save facility"}
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
      {dialog}
    </>
  );
}


=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminFeedback.tsx ===
import { useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { format } from "date-fns";
import { Reply, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { apiDelete, apiGet, apiPatch } from "@/lib/api";
import {
  FEEDBACK_KINDS,
  FEEDBACK_STATUSES,
  type DeleteResult,
  type Feedback,
  type FeedbackReplyIn,
  type FeedbackStatus,
} from "@/lib/adminTypes";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { PageHeader, useConfirm } from "@/components/admin/AdminUi";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const FILTERS: { value: "" | FeedbackStatus; label: string }[] = [
  { value: "", label: "All" },
  { value: "open", label: "Open" },
  { value: "in_progress", label: "In progress" },
  { value: "resolved", label: "Resolved" },
];

export default function AdminFeedback() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { confirm, dialog } = useConfirm();
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<"" | FeedbackStatus>("");
  const [replying, setReplying] = useState<Feedback | null>(null);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<FeedbackStatus>("in_progress");
  const [reply, setReply] = useState("");

  const list = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "feedback", filter],
    queryFn: () => apiGet<Feedback[]>(`/admin/feedback${filter ? `?status=${filter}` : ""}`),
  });

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, body }: { id: string; body: FeedbackReplyIn }) => apiPatch<Feedback>(`/admin/feedback/${id}`, body),
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
      setReplying(null);
      toast.success("Reply saved — the citizen sees it in their Feedback window");
    },
    onError: () => toast.error("Could not save the reply"),
  });

  const remove = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => apiDelete<DeleteResult>(`/admin/feedback/${id}`),
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
      toast.success("Feedback deleted");
    },
    onError: () => toast.error("Could not delete"),
  });

  const openReply = (f: Feedback) => {
    setReplying(f);
    setStatus(f.status === "open" ? "in_progress" : f.status);
    setReply(f.admin_reply);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Feedback & complaints" subtitle="Messages citizens send with the Feedback button. Reply and track each one to resolution." />

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2" role="group" aria-label="Filter feedback by status">
        {FILTERS.map((f) => (
          <button
            key={f.value || "all"}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setFilter(f.value)}
            data-testid={`feedback-filter-${f.value || "all"}`}
            className={cn(
              "rounded-full border px-3.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors",
              filter === f.value ? "border-emerald-700 bg-emerald-700 text-white" : "border-emerald-900/15 bg-white text-emerald-800 hover:bg-emerald-50",
            )}
          >
            {f.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {list.isLoading && <p className="text-sm text-emerald-950/60">Loading…</p>}
      {list.data?.length === 0 && (
        <Card className="border-dashed border-emerald-950/15">
          <CardContent className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-emerald-950/60" data-testid="admin-feedback-empty">
            No feedback here yet. Citizens send it with the Feedback button in the app header.
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-3">
        {(list.data ?? []).map((f) => {
          const st = FEEDBACK_STATUSES.find((s) => s.value === f.status);
          return (
            <Card key={f.id} className="border-emerald-950/10" data-testid={`admin-feedback-card-${f.id}`}>
              <CardContent className="space-y-2 pt-5">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-emerald-700/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-800">
                      {FEEDBACK_KINDS.find((k) => k.value === f.kind)?.label}
                    </span>
                    <span className={`rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${st?.cls ?? ""}`} data-testid={`admin-feedback-status-${f.id}`}>
                      {st?.label}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-emerald-950/60">{f.user_name || f.user_email} · {format(new Date(f.created_at), "dd MMM yyyy, HH:mm")}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => openReply(f)} data-testid={`admin-feedback-reply-${f.id}`}>
                      <Reply className="mr-1 h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden /> Reply / update
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      size="icon-sm"
                      variant="ghost"
                      aria-label="Delete feedback"
                      onClick={() => confirm("Delete this feedback permanently?", () => remove.mutate(f.id))}
                      data-testid={`admin-feedback-delete-${f.id}`}
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 text-red-600" aria-hidden />
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-emerald-950/85">{f.message}</p>
                {f.admin_reply && (
                  <p className="rounded-lg bg-[#F4F7F4] p-2.5 text-sm text-emerald-900" data-testid={`admin-feedback-saved-reply-${f.id}`}>
                    <span className="font-semibold">Your reply:</span> {f.admin_reply}
                  </p>
                )}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <Dialog open={replying !== null} onOpenChange={(o: boolean) => !o && setReplying(null)}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg" data-testid="feedback-reply-dialog">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="font-heading">Reply to {replying?.user_name || replying?.user_email}</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <p className="rounded-lg bg-[#F4F7F4] p-3 text-sm">{replying?.message}</p>
          <Select value={status} onValueChange={(v: string) => setStatus(v as FeedbackStatus)}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-full" data-testid="feedback-reply-status-select">
              <SelectValue>{(v: string) => FEEDBACK_STATUSES.find((s) => s.value === v)?.label ?? v}</SelectValue>
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              {FEEDBACK_STATUSES.map((s) => (
                <SelectItem key={s.value} value={s.value} data-testid={`feedback-reply-status-${s.value}`}>{s.label}</SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
          <Textarea rows={4} value={reply} onChange={(e) => setReply(e.target.value)} placeholder="Write your reply to the citizen…" data-testid="feedback-reply-input" />
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setReplying(null)} data-testid="feedback-reply-cancel">Cancel</Button>
            <Button
              disabled={save.isPending}
              onClick={() => replying && save.mutate({ id: replying.id, body: { status, admin_reply: reply.trim() } })}
              data-testid="feedback-reply-save"
            >
              {save.isPending ? "Saving…" : "Save reply"}
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
      {dialog}
    </>
  );
}


=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminGuidelines.tsx ===
import { useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Pencil, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { apiDelete, apiPost, apiPut } from "@/lib/api";
import { errorMessage, type DeleteResult, type Guideline, type GuidelineIn } from "@/lib/adminTypes";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { CategoryPill, PageHeader, commas, lines, useConfirm } from "@/components/admin/AdminUi";
import { useGuidelines } from "@/components/admin/useGuidelines";

interface FormState {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  examples: string;
  disposal_steps: string;
  hazards: string;
  eco_points: string;
  color: string;
  active: boolean;
}

const EMPTY: FormState = {
  key: "",
  name: "",
  description: "",
  examples: "",
  disposal_steps: "",
  hazards: "",
  eco_points: "10",
  color: "#0EA5E9",
  active: true,
};

function toForm(g: Guideline): FormState {
  return {
    key: g.key,
    name: g.name,
    description: g.description,
    examples: g.examples.join(", "),
    disposal_steps: g.disposal_steps.join("\n"),
    hazards: g.hazards.join("\n"),
    eco_points: String(g.eco_points),
    color: g.color,
    active: g.active,
  };
}

export default function AdminGuidelines() {
  const g = useGuidelines();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { confirm, dialog } = useConfirm();
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Guideline | null>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState<FormState>(EMPTY);
  const set = <K extends keyof FormState>(k: K, v: FormState[K]) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: v }));

  const done = (msg: string) => {
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
    setOpen(false);
    toast.success(msg);
  };

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: GuidelineIn) =>
      editing ? apiPut<Guideline>(`/admin/guidelines/${editing.id}`, body) : apiPost<Guideline>("/admin/guidelines", body),
    onSuccess: () => done(editing ? "Category updated" : "Category added"),
    onError: (e) => toast.error(errorMessage(e, "Could not save — check the fields")),
  });

  const remove = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => apiDelete<DeleteResult>(`/admin/guidelines/${id}`),
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
      toast.success("Category deleted");
    },
    onError: (e) => toast.error(errorMessage(e, "Could not delete")),
  });

  const openNew = () => {
    setEditing(null);
    setForm(EMPTY);
    setOpen(true);
  };
  const openEdit = (item: Guideline) => {
    setEditing(item);
    setForm(toForm(item));
    setOpen(true);
  };

  const submit = () => {
    save.mutate({
      key: form.key.trim().toLowerCase(),
      name: form.name.trim(),
      description: form.description.trim(),
      examples: commas(form.examples),
      disposal_steps: lines(form.disposal_steps),
      hazards: lines(form.hazards),
      eco_points: Number(form.eco_points) || 0,
      color: form.color,
      active: form.active,
    });
  };

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Waste categories & disposal guidelines"
        subtitle="What you write here is used by the AI when citizens scan items and ask the assistant."
        action={
          <Button onClick={openNew} data-testid="categories-add-button">
            <Plus className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> Add category
          </Button>
        }
      />

      {g.isLoading && <p className="text-sm text-emerald-950/60">Loading…</p>}
      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        {(g.data ?? []).map((item) => (
          <Card key={item.id} className="border-emerald-950/10" data-testid={`category-card-${item.key}`}>
            <CardContent className="space-y-3 pt-5">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <CategoryPill label={item.name} color={item.color} />
                  <code className="text-xs text-emerald-950/50">{item.key}</code>
                  {item.active ? (
                    <Badge variant="secondary" data-testid={`category-status-${item.key}`}>Active</Badge>
                  ) : (
                    <Badge variant="outline" data-testid={`category-status-${item.key}`}>Hidden</Badge>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="flex shrink-0">
                  <Button size="icon-sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Edit ${item.name}`} onClick={() => openEdit(item)} data-testid={`category-edit-${item.key}`}>
                    <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
                  </Button>
                  {item.key !== "general" && (
                    <Button
                      size="icon-sm"
                      variant="ghost"
                      aria-label={`Delete ${item.name}`}
                      onClick={() => confirm(`Delete the category "${item.name}"? Past records keep their label.`, () => remove.mutate(item.id))}
                      data-testid={`category-delete-${item.key}`}
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 text-red-600" aria-hidden />
                    </Button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-emerald-950/75">{item.description}</p>
              {item.disposal_steps.length > 0 && (
                <ol className="list-decimal space-y-0.5 pl-5 text-sm text-emerald-950/80">
                  {item.disposal_steps.map((s, i) => <li key={i}>{s}</li>)}
                </ol>
              )}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs text-emerald-950/55">
                <span data-testid={`category-points-${item.key}`}>+{item.eco_points} eco points</span>
                <span>{item.scan_count} scans</span>
                <span>{item.facility_count} facilities</span>
                {item.examples.length > 0 && <span>e.g. {item.examples.slice(0, 3).join(", ")}</span>}
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-xl" data-testid="category-form-dialog">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="font-heading">{editing ? `Edit ${editing.name}` : "Add a waste category"}</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="cat-name">Name</Label>
              <Input id="cat-name" value={form.name} onChange={(e) => set("name", e.target.value)} placeholder="Textile" data-testid="category-name-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="cat-key">Key (lowercase, fixed)</Label>
              <Input
                id="cat-key"
                value={form.key}
                disabled={!!editing}
                onChange={(e) => set("key", e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_]/g, ""))}
                placeholder="textile"
                data-testid="category-key-input"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
              <Label htmlFor="cat-desc">Description (what belongs here)</Label>
              <Textarea id="cat-desc" rows={2} value={form.description} onChange={(e) => set("description", e.target.value)} data-testid="category-description-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
              <Label htmlFor="cat-examples">Examples (comma separated)</Label>
              <Input id="cat-examples" value={form.examples} onChange={(e) => set("examples", e.target.value)} placeholder="Old clothes, bedsheets, shoes" data-testid="category-examples-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
              <Label htmlFor="cat-steps">Disposal steps (one per line)</Label>
              <Textarea id="cat-steps" rows={4} value={form.disposal_steps} onChange={(e) => set("disposal_steps", e.target.value)} data-testid="category-steps-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
              <Label htmlFor="cat-hazards">Hazards (one per line, optional)</Label>
              <Textarea id="cat-hazards" rows={2} value={form.hazards} onChange={(e) => set("hazards", e.target.value)} data-testid="category-hazards-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="cat-points">Eco points per scan</Label>
              <Input id="cat-points" type="number" min={0} max={100} value={form.eco_points} onChange={(e) => set("eco_points", e.target.value)} data-testid="category-points-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="cat-color">Colour</Label>
              <Input id="cat-color" type="color" value={form.color} onChange={(e) => set("color", e.target.value)} className="h-9 p-1" data-testid="category-color-input" />
            </div>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm sm:col-span-2">
              <Checkbox checked={form.active} onCheckedChange={(c: boolean) => set("active", c)} data-testid="category-active-checkbox" />
              Active — the AI may classify items into this category
            </label>
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setOpen(false)} data-testid="category-cancel-button">Cancel</Button>
            <Button onClick={submit} disabled={save.isPending || form.name.trim().length < 2 || form.key.trim().length < 2} data-testid="category-save-button">
              {save.isPending ? "Saving…" : "Save category"}
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
      {dialog}
    </>
  );
}


=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminLayout.tsx ===
import { Link, Navigate, NavLink, Outlet } from "react-router-dom";
import { UserButton, useAuth } from "@clerk/react";
import {
  ClipboardList,
  Lightbulb,
  Building2,
  History,
  LayoutDashboard,
  MessageSquareWarning,
  Recycle,
  Settings,
  Truck,
  ShieldAlert,
  Tags,
  Users,
} from "lucide-react";

import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import AdminPasscodeGate from "@/components/admin/AdminPasscodeGate";
import { apiGet } from "@/lib/api";
import type { AdminGate } from "@/lib/adminTypes";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/sonner";
import { useMe } from "@/lib/useMe";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const NAV = [
  { to: "/admin", label: "Dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard, testid: "admin-nav-dashboard", end: true },
  { to: "/admin/records", label: "Waste records", icon: ClipboardList, testid: "admin-nav-records" },
  { to: "/admin/categories", label: "Categories & rules", icon: Tags, testid: "admin-nav-categories" },
  { to: "/admin/facilities", label: "Facilities", icon: Building2, testid: "admin-nav-facilities" },
  { to: "/admin/users", label: "Users", icon: Users, testid: "admin-nav-users" },
  { to: "/admin/tips", label: "Tips & knowledge", icon: Lightbulb, testid: "admin-nav-tips" },
  { to: "/admin/feedback", label: "Feedback", icon: MessageSquareWarning, testid: "admin-nav-feedback" },
  { to: "/admin/audit", label: "Audit log", icon: History, testid: "admin-nav-audit" },
  { to: "/admin/doorstep", label: "Doorstep Demo", icon: Truck, testid: "admin-nav-doorstep" },
  { to: "/admin/settings", label: "Settings", icon: Settings, testid: "admin-nav-settings" },
];

export default function AdminLayout() {
  const { isLoaded, isSignedIn } = useAuth();
  const me = useMe();
  const isAdmin = me.data?.role === "admin";
  const gate = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "gate"],
    queryFn: () => apiGet<AdminGate>("/admin/gate"),
    enabled: !!isSignedIn && isAdmin,
  });

  if (!isLoaded || (isSignedIn && me.isLoading) || (isAdmin && gate.isLoading)) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-svh items-center justify-center bg-[#0D1B13] text-emerald-50">
        <Recycle className="h-8 w-8 animate-pulse" aria-label="Loading" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (!isSignedIn) {
    return <Navigate to={`/sign-in?redirect_url=${encodeURIComponent(`${window.location.origin}/admin`)}`} replace />;
  }

  if (me.data?.role !== "admin") {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-svh flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 bg-[#F4F7F4] px-4 text-center" data-testid="admin-access-denied">
        <ShieldAlert className="h-12 w-12 text-red-600" aria-hidden />
        <h1 className="font-heading text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-950">Admin access only</h1>
        <p className="max-w-sm text-sm text-emerald-950/65">
          This area is for the Chiplun town administrator. Your account does not have admin rights.
        </p>
        <Button render={<Link to="/" />} data-testid="admin-denied-home-link">
          Back to Swachh Chiplun
        </Button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (gate.data && !gate.data.unlocked) return <AdminPasscodeGate gate={gate.data} />;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-svh bg-[#F4F7F4] md:grid md:grid-cols-[240px_1fr]">
      <aside className="hidden flex-col bg-[#0D1B13] text-emerald-50 md:sticky md:top-0 md:flex md:h-svh">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-5 py-5">
          <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-emerald-500 text-emerald-950">
            <Recycle className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden />
          </span>
          <div className="leading-tight">
            <p className="font-heading text-base font-bold">Swachh Chiplun</p>
            <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-300">Admin console</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <nav className="flex-1 space-y-1 px-3" aria-label="Admin navigation">
          {NAV.map((item) => (
            <NavLink
              key={item.to}
              to={item.to}
              end={item.end}
              data-testid={item.testid}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                cn(
                  "flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors",
                  isActive ? "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-200" : "text-emerald-50/75 hover:bg-white/5",
                )
              }
            >
              <item.icon className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
              {item.label}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="space-y-3 border-t border-white/10 px-5 py-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <UserButton />
            <div className="min-w-0 leading-tight">
              <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold" data-testid="admin-current-name">{me.data?.name}</p>
              <p className="truncate text-[11px] text-emerald-200/70">{me.data?.email}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <Link to="/" className="block text-xs text-emerald-300 hover:underline" data-testid="admin-open-citizen-app">
            ← Open citizen app
          </Link>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="min-w-0">
        <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-[#0D1B13] px-4 py-3 text-emerald-50 md:hidden">
          <p className="font-heading font-bold">Swachh Chiplun · Admin</p>
          <UserButton />
        </header>
        <nav className="flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto border-b border-emerald-950/10 bg-white px-3 py-2 md:hidden" aria-label="Admin mobile navigation">
          {NAV.map((item) => (
            <NavLink
              key={item.to}
              to={item.to}
              end={item.end}
              data-testid={`${item.testid}-mobile`}
              className={({ isActive }) =>
                cn(
                  "shrink-0 rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold",
                  isActive ? "bg-emerald-700 text-white" : "bg-[#F4F7F4] text-emerald-900",
                )
              }
            >
              {item.label}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-6xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 md:px-8 md:py-8">
          <Outlet />
        </main>
      </div>
      <Toaster richColors position="bottom-right" />
    </div>
  );
}


=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminRecords.tsx ===
import { useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { format } from "date-fns";
import { Download, Eye, Search, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { apiDelete, apiGet } from "@/lib/api";
import type { AdminScan, DeleteResult, ScanPage } from "@/lib/adminTypes";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "@/components/ui/table";
import { CategoryPill, PageHeader, useConfirm } from "@/components/admin/AdminUi";
import { useGuidelines } from "@/components/admin/useGuidelines";

const PERIODS = [
  { value: "0", label: "All time" },
  { value: "1", label: "Last 24 hours" },
  { value: "7", label: "Last 7 days" },
  { value: "30", label: "Last 30 days" },
];

function toCsv(rows: AdminScan[]): string {
  const esc = (v: unknown) => `"${String(v ?? "").replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
  const head = ["date", "item", "category", "confidence", "eco_points", "user", "language", "hint"];
  const body = rows.map((r) =>
    [r.created_at, r.item_name, r.category, r.confidence.toFixed(2), r.eco_points, r.user_email, r.language, r.hint].map(esc).join(","),
  );
  return [head.join(","), ...body].join("\n");
}

export default function AdminRecords() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const userFilter = params.get("user") ?? "";
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("");
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState("all");
  const [days, setDays] = useState("0");
  const [lowOnly, setLowOnly] = useState(false);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [viewing, setViewing] = useState<AdminScan | null>(null);
  const g = useGuidelines();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { confirm, dialog } = useConfirm();

  const query = new URLSearchParams({ q, page: String(page), page_size: "25", days });
  if (category !== "all") query.set("category", category);
  if (lowOnly) query.set("low_confidence", "true");
  if (userFilter) query.set("user_id", userFilter);

  const records = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "scans", query.toString()],
    queryFn: () => apiGet<ScanPage>(`/admin/scans?${query.toString()}`),
  });

  const remove = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => apiDelete<DeleteResult>(`/admin/scans/${id}`),
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
      toast.success("Record deleted");
    },
    onError: () => toast.error("Could not delete the record"),
  });

  const total = records.data?.total ?? 0;
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / 25));

  const exportCsv = async () => {
    // export ALL records matching the current filters (up to 200 per file)
    const all = new URLSearchParams(query);
    all.set("page", "1");
    all.set("page_size", "200");
    const rows = (await apiGet<ScanPage>(`/admin/scans?${all.toString()}`)).items;
    const blob = new Blob([toCsv(rows)], { type: "text/csv" });
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.download = "swachh-chiplun-records.csv";
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Waste identification records"
        subtitle="Every scan made by citizens — search, filter, inspect or remove."
        action={
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={exportCsv} disabled={!records.data?.items.length} data-testid="records-export-csv-button">
            <Download className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> Export CSV
          </Button>
        }
      />

      <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
        <CardContent className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 pt-5">
          <form
            className="flex min-w-60 flex-1 gap-2"
            onSubmit={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              setQ(draft.trim());
              setPage(1);
            }}
          >
            <Input value={draft} onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search item name or hint…" data-testid="records-search-input" />
            <Button type="submit" data-testid="records-search-button">
              <Search className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
              <span className="sr-only">Search</span>
            </Button>
          </form>
          <Select value={category} onValueChange={(v: string) => { setCategory(v); setPage(1); }}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-44" data-testid="records-category-filter">
              <SelectValue>{(v: string) => (v === "all" ? "All categories" : g.label(v))}</SelectValue>
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              <SelectItem value="all" data-testid="records-category-all">All categories</SelectItem>
              {(g.data ?? []).map((c) => (
                <SelectItem key={c.key} value={c.key} data-testid={`records-category-${c.key}`}>
                  {c.name}
                </SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
          <Select value={days} onValueChange={(v: string) => { setDays(v); setPage(1); }}>
            <SelectTrigger className="w-40" data-testid="records-period-filter">
              <SelectValue>{(v: string) => PERIODS.find((p) => p.value === v)?.label ?? v}</SelectValue>
            </SelectTrigger>
            <SelectContent>
              {PERIODS.map((p) => (
                <SelectItem key={p.value} value={p.value} data-testid={`records-period-${p.value}`}>
                  {p.label}
                </SelectItem>
              ))}
            </SelectContent>
          </Select>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-emerald-950/80">
            <Checkbox checked={lowOnly} onCheckedChange={(c: boolean) => { setLowOnly(c); setPage(1); }} data-testid="records-low-confidence-filter" />
            Low confidence only
          </label>
          {userFilter && (
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setParams({})} data-testid="records-clear-user-filter">
              Showing one user · clear
            </Button>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
        <CardContent className="pt-5">
          <p className="mb-3 text-sm text-emerald-950/60" data-testid="records-total">
            {records.isLoading ? "Loading…" : `${total} record${total === 1 ? "" : "s"} found`}
          </p>
          <Table>
            <TableHeader>
              <TableRow>
                <TableHead>Item</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Category</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Confidence</TableHead>
                <TableHead>User</TableHead>
                <TableHead>When</TableHead>
                <TableHead aria-label="Actions" />
              </TableRow>
            </TableHeader>
            <TableBody>
              {(records.data?.items ?? []).map((r) => (
                <TableRow key={r.id} data-testid={`records-row-${r.id}`}>
                  <TableCell className="font-medium">{r.item_name}</TableCell>
                  <TableCell>
                    <CategoryPill label={r.category_label || g.label(r.category)} color={g.color(r.category)} />
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell className={r.confidence < 0.65 ? "font-semibold text-amber-700" : ""}>{Math.round(r.confidence * 100)}%</TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="max-w-48 truncate text-emerald-950/70">{r.user_email || "demo (before sign-in)"}</TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap text-emerald-950/60">{format(new Date(r.created_at), "dd MMM yyyy, HH:mm")}</TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap text-right">
                    <Button size="icon-sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setViewing(r)} aria-label="View record" data-testid={`records-view-${r.id}`}>
                      <Eye className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      size="icon-sm"
                      variant="ghost"
                      aria-label="Delete record"
                      onClick={() => confirm(`Delete the record "${r.item_name}"? This cannot be undone.`, () => remove.mutate(r.id))}
                      data-testid={`records-delete-${r.id}`}
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 text-red-600" aria-hidden />
                    </Button>
                  </TableCell>
                </TableRow>
              ))}
            </TableBody>
          </Table>
          {records.data && records.data.items.length === 0 && (
            <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-emerald-950/60" data-testid="records-empty">No records match these filters.</p>
          )}
          <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between">
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p - 1)} data-testid="records-prev-page">
              Previous
            </Button>
            <span className="text-sm text-emerald-950/60" data-testid="records-page-indicator">Page {page} of {pages}</span>
            <Button variant="outline" size="sm" disabled={page >= pages} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p + 1)} data-testid="records-next-page">
              Next
            </Button>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Dialog open={viewing !== null} onOpenChange={(o: boolean) => !o && setViewing(null)}>
        <DialogContent className="max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-lg" data-testid="records-detail-dialog">
          {viewing && (
            <>
              <DialogHeader>
                <DialogTitle className="font-heading">{viewing.item_name}</DialogTitle>
              </DialogHeader>
              <div className="space-y-3 text-sm">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <CategoryPill label={viewing.category_label || g.label(viewing.category)} color={g.color(viewing.category)} />
                  <span className="text-emerald-950/60">{Math.round(viewing.confidence * 100)}% confidence · +{viewing.eco_points} pts · {viewing.language.toUpperCase()}</span>
                </div>
                <p><span className="font-semibold">User:</span> {viewing.user_email || "demo (before sign-in)"}</p>
                {viewing.hint && <p><span className="font-semibold">Hint typed:</span> {viewing.hint}</p>}
                {viewing.hazards.length > 0 && (
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-semibold text-red-700">Hazards</p>
                    <ul className="list-disc pl-5">{viewing.hazards.map((h, i) => <li key={i}>{h}</li>)}</ul>
                  </div>
                )}
                <div>
                  <p className="font-semibold">Disposal steps given</p>
                  <ol className="list-decimal pl-5">{viewing.disposal_steps.map((s, i) => <li key={i}>{s}</li>)}</ol>
                </div>
                {viewing.needs_follow_up && <p className="rounded-lg bg-amber-50 p-2 text-amber-800">Follow-up asked: {viewing.follow_up_question}</p>}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
      {dialog}
    </>
  );
}


=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminSettings.tsx ===
import { useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { AlertTriangle } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { apiGet, apiPost, ApiError } from "@/lib/api";
import type { DemoDataCounts } from "@/lib/doorstepTypes";
import { PageHeader, useConfirm } from "@/components/admin/AdminUi";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";

const PHRASE = "CLEAR DEMO";

export default function AdminSettings() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { confirm, dialog } = useConfirm();
  const counts = useQuery({ queryKey: ["demo-data-counts"], queryFn: () => apiGet<DemoDataCounts>("/admin/demo-data") });
  const [phrase, setPhrase] = useState("");
  const [sure, setSure] = useState(false);
  const clear = useMutation({
