    mutationFn: () => apiPost<DemoDataCounts>("/admin/demo-data/clear", { phrase, confirm: sure }),
    onSuccess: (r) => {
      toast.success(`Deleted ${r.demo_users} demo users and ${r.demo_trips} demo trips`);
      setPhrase("");
      setSure(false);
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["demo-data-counts"] });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["doorstep-state"] });
      void qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["doorstep-demo-users"] });
    },
    onError: (e) => toast.error(e instanceof ApiError || e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Could not clear demo data"),
  });
  const ready = phrase === PHRASE && sure && !clear.isPending;
  const c = counts.data;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {dialog}
      <PageHeader title="Settings" subtitle="Admin settings for Swachh Chiplun." />

      <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
        <CardContent className="pt-5 text-sm text-muted-foreground" data-testid="settings-general-info">
          The admin is the single verified email set in <code>ADMIN_EMAIL</code>. Categories, facilities and tips are managed from their own pages.
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <section className="rounded-2xl border-2 border-red-200 bg-red-50/50 p-5" data-testid="settings-danger-zone">
        <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 font-heading text-lg font-bold text-red-800">
          <AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden /> Danger zone
        </h2>
        <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
          <div>
            <p className="font-semibold text-red-950">Clear Doorstep demo data</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-red-900" data-testid="settings-demo-counts">
              This will delete <b>{c?.demo_users ?? "…"}</b> demo users and <b>{c?.demo_trips ?? "…"}</b> demo trips. Real users, their scans and their saved pickup spots are never touched.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="max-w-sm space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="clear-phrase">Type {PHRASE} to continue</Label>
            <Input id="clear-phrase" value={phrase} onChange={(e) => setPhrase(e.target.value)} autoComplete="off" data-testid="settings-clear-phrase-input" />
          </div>
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-red-950">
            <input type="checkbox" className="h-4 w-4 accent-red-700" checked={sure} onChange={(e) => setSure(e.target.checked)} data-testid="settings-clear-confirm-checkbox" />
            I understand this cannot be undone.
          </label>
          <Button
            variant="destructive"
            disabled={!ready}
            onClick={() => confirm(`Delete ${c?.demo_users ?? 0} demo users and ${c?.demo_trips ?? 0} demo trips?`, () => clear.mutate())}
            data-testid="settings-clear-demo-button"
          >
            Clear demo data
          </Button>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}


=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminTips.tsx ===
import { useState } from "react";
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Pencil, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

import { apiDelete, apiGet, apiPost, apiPut } from "@/lib/api";
import { errorMessage, type DeleteResult, type Tip, type TipIn } from "@/lib/adminTypes";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { CategoryPill, PageHeader, useConfirm } from "@/components/admin/AdminUi";
import { useGuidelines } from "@/components/admin/useGuidelines";

const EMPTY: TipIn = { title: "", body: "", category: "", active: true };

export default function AdminTips() {
  const g = useGuidelines();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { confirm, dialog } = useConfirm();
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Tip | null>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [form, setForm] = useState<TipIn>(EMPTY);

  const tips = useQuery({ queryKey: ["admin", "tips"], queryFn: () => apiGet<Tip[]>("/admin/tips") });

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: TipIn) => (editing ? apiPut<Tip>(`/admin/tips/${editing.id}`, body) : apiPost<Tip>("/admin/tips", body)),
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
      setOpen(false);
      toast.success(editing ? "Tip updated" : "Tip added");
    },
    onError: (e) => toast.error(errorMessage(e, "Could not save — check the fields")),
  });

  const remove = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => apiDelete<DeleteResult>(`/admin/tips/${id}`),
    onSuccess: () => {
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["admin"] });
      toast.success("Tip deleted");
    },
    onError: () => toast.error("Could not delete"),
  });

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Disposal tips & knowledge"
        subtitle="Short tips the AI assistant uses when answering citizens' questions."
        action={
          <Button onClick={() => { setEditing(null); setForm(EMPTY); setOpen(true); }} data-testid="tips-add-button">
            <Plus className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" aria-hidden /> Add tip
          </Button>
        }
      />
      {tips.data?.length === 0 && (
        <Card className="border-dashed border-emerald-950/15">
          <CardContent className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-emerald-950/60" data-testid="tips-empty">
            No tips yet. Add local knowledge, e.g. "Ward-wise dry waste is collected every Wednesday".
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}
      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        {(tips.data ?? []).map((t) => (
          <Card key={t.id} className="border-emerald-950/10" data-testid={`tip-card-${t.id}`}>
            <CardContent className="space-y-2 pt-5">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                  <h3 className="font-heading font-bold text-emerald-950">{t.title}</h3>
                  {t.category && <CategoryPill label={g.label(t.category)} color={g.color(t.category)} />}
                  {!t.active && <Badge variant="outline">Hidden</Badge>}
                </div>
                <div className="flex shrink-0">
                  <Button size="icon-sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Edit ${t.title}`} onClick={() => { setEditing(t); setForm({ title: t.title, body: t.body, category: t.category, active: t.active }); setOpen(true); }} data-testid={`tip-edit-${t.id}`}>
                    <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" aria-hidden />
                  </Button>
                  <Button size="icon-sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Delete ${t.title}`} onClick={() => confirm(`Delete the tip "${t.title}"?`, () => remove.mutate(t.id))} data-testid={`tip-delete-${t.id}`}>
                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4 text-red-600" aria-hidden />
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <p className="whitespace-pre-line text-sm text-emerald-950/75">{t.body}</p>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-lg" data-testid="tip-form-dialog">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="font-heading">{editing ? "Edit tip" : "Add a disposal tip"}</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="tip-title">Title</Label>
              <Input id="tip-title" value={form.title} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, title: e.target.value })} data-testid="tip-title-input" />
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <Label htmlFor="tip-body">Tip</Label>
              <Textarea id="tip-body" rows={4} value={form.body} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, body: e.target.value })} data-testid="tip-body-input" />
            </div>
            <Select value={form.category || "none"} onValueChange={(v: string) => setForm({ ...form, category: v === "none" ? "" : v })}>
              <SelectTrigger className="w-full" data-testid="tip-category-select">
                <SelectValue>{(v: string) => (v === "none" ? "Any category" : g.label(v))}</SelectValue>
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="none">Any category</SelectItem>
                {(g.data ?? []).map((c) => <SelectItem key={c.key} value={c.key}>{c.name}</SelectItem>)}
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
              <Checkbox checked={form.active} onCheckedChange={(c: boolean) => setForm({ ...form, active: c })} data-testid="tip-active-checkbox" />
              Active — the assistant may use this tip
            </label>
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setOpen(false)} data-testid="tip-cancel-button">Cancel</Button>
            <Button disabled={save.isPending || form.title.trim().length < 2 || form.body.trim().length < 2} onClick={() => save.mutate({ ...form, title: form.title.trim(), body: form.body.trim() })} data-testid="tip-save-button">
              {save.isPending ? "Saving…" : "Save tip"}
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
      {dialog}
    </>
  );
}


=== FILE: frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminUsers.tsx ===
import { useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { format, formatDistanceToNow } from "date-fns";
import { ExternalLink, Info, Search, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";

import { apiGet } from "@/lib/api";
import type { UserRow, UsersResponse } from "@/lib/adminTypes";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Table, TableBody, TableCell, TableHead, TableHeader, TableRow } from "@/components/ui/table";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/admin/AdminUi";

// Clerk dashboard deep link — block / unblock / delete the sign-in account there (free).
const clerkUserUrl = (id: string) => `https://dashboard.clerk.com/last-active?path=users/${id}`;

export default function AdminUsers() {
  const users = useQuery({ queryKey: ["admin", "users"], queryFn: () => apiGet<UsersResponse>("/admin/users") });
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [viewing, setViewing] = useState<UserRow | null>(null);

  const needle = q.trim().toLowerCase();
  const rows = (users.data?.users ?? []).filter(
    (u) => !needle || u.email.toLowerCase().includes(needle) || u.name.toLowerCase().includes(needle),
  );

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Users" subtitle="Everyone who signed in to Swachh Chiplun, with their activity." />

      <Card className="border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/60">
        <CardContent className="flex items-start gap-3 pt-5 text-sm text-amber-900" data-testid="users-block-info">
          <Info className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
          <p>
            <span className="font-semibold">Block, unblock or delete a user:</span> open the user and press{" "}
            <span className="font-semibold">Open in Clerk</span>. Clerk (the sign-in service) blocks them everywhere with one
            click. There is exactly one admin (ADMIN_EMAIL), so there is no "make admin" option.
          </p>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card className="border-emerald-950/10">
        <CardContent className="pt-5">
          <div className="relative mb-4 max-w-sm">
            <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-emerald-950/40" aria-hidden />
            <Input value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search name or email…" className="pl-8" data-testid="users-search-input" />
          </div>
          <Table>
            <TableHeader>
              <TableRow>
                <TableHead>User</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Role</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Scans</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Joined</TableHead>
                <TableHead>Last active</TableHead>
                <TableHead aria-label="Actions" />
              </TableRow>
            </TableHeader>
            <TableBody>
              {rows.map((u) => (
                <TableRow key={u.user_id} data-testid={`user-row-${u.user_id}`}>
                  <TableCell>
                    <p className="font-medium">{u.name || u.email}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-emerald-950/55">{u.email}</p>
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell data-testid={`user-role-${u.user_id}`}>
                    {u.role === "admin" ? (
                      <Badge className="bg-emerald-700 text-white"><ShieldCheck className="mr-1 h-3 w-3" aria-hidden />Admin</Badge>
                    ) : (
                      <Badge variant="secondary">User</Badge>
                    )}
                  </TableCell>
                  <TableCell data-testid={`user-scan-count-${u.user_id}`}>{u.scan_count}</TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap text-emerald-950/60">{u.created_at ? format(new Date(u.created_at), "dd MMM yyyy") : "—"}</TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="whitespace-nowrap text-emerald-950/60">{u.last_seen ? formatDistanceToNow(new Date(u.last_seen), { addSuffix: true }) : "—"}</TableCell>
                  <TableCell className="text-right">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setViewing(u)} data-testid={`user-view-${u.user_id}`}>
                      Details
                    </Button>
                  </TableCell>
                </TableRow>
              ))}
            </TableBody>
          </Table>
          {users.data && rows.length === 0 && <p className="py-6 text-center text-sm text-emerald-950/60" data-testid="users-empty">No users match.</p>}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Dialog open={viewing !== null} onOpenChange={(o: boolean) => !o && setViewing(null)}>
        <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-md" data-testid="user-detail-dialog">
          {viewing && (
            <>
              <DialogHeader>
                <DialogTitle className="font-heading">{viewing.name || viewing.email}</DialogTitle>
              </DialogHeader>
              <dl className="grid grid-cols-[110px_1fr] gap-y-2 text-sm">
                <dt className="text-emerald-950/55">Email</dt><dd>{viewing.email}</dd>
                <dt className="text-emerald-950/55">Role</dt><dd>{viewing.role === "admin" ? "Admin (ADMIN_EMAIL)" : "User"}</dd>
                <dt className="text-emerald-950/55">Scans</dt><dd>{viewing.scan_count}</dd>
                <dt className="text-emerald-950/55">Feedback</dt><dd>{viewing.feedback_count}</dd>
                <dt className="text-emerald-950/55">Joined</dt><dd>{viewing.created_at ? format(new Date(viewing.created_at), "dd MMM yyyy, HH:mm") : "—"}</dd>
                <dt className="text-emerald-950/55">User ID</dt><dd className="break-all font-mono text-xs">{viewing.user_id}</dd>
              </dl>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 pt-2">
                <Button render={<Link to={`/admin/records?user=${viewing.user_id}`} />} data-testid="user-detail-scans">
                  View scan history
                </Button>
                <Button
                  variant="outline"
                  render={<a href={clerkUserUrl(viewing.user_id)} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" />}
                  data-testid="user-detail-open-clerk"
                >
                  Open in Clerk (block / delete) <ExternalLink className="ml-1 h-3.5 w-3.5" aria-hidden />
                </Button>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </>
  );
}
